# PART 03 — TONE 3: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*[Engraving: The Crucifixion of Christ with the Mother of God and St. John the Theologian, flanked by floral baroque foliage.]*

*On Saturday Evening, at Little Vespers.*

*At "Lord, I have cried," resurrectional stichera, 3, repeating the first.*

*Tone 3.*

By Thy Cross, O Christ Savior, the dominion of death was dissolved, and the delusion of the devil was brought to naught; and the human race, being saved by faith, ever offers a hymn unto Thee.[^2263]

All things were enlightened by Thy Resurrection, O Lord, and Paradise was opened once again; and all creation praising Thee, ever offers a hymn unto Thee.[^2264]

I glorify the power of the Father and of the Son, and I praise the authority of the Holy Spirit: the undivided, uncreated Godhead, the consubstantial Trinity, reigning unto ages of ages.[^2265]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion, Tone 3:*
A most great miracle! The Virgin hath given birth, and the One born is God before the ages: a manifestation of birth, and accomplished beyond nature! O awesome mystery! which even when contemplated remains ineffable, and when beheld cannot be comprehended! Blessed art thou, O all-pure Maiden, daughter of earthly Adam, and Mother of God Most High Who appeared: Him do thou entreat, that our souls may be saved.[^2266]

*Then: O Gladsome Light. After this, the Prokeimenon: "The Lord is King" (three times). Verse: "The Lord is clothed with strength, and hath girded Himself" (Psalm 92:1). Then: "Vouchsafe, O Lord, to keep us this evening." But the priest says no litany; rather we sing:*[^2267]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrectional Sticheron, Tone 3:*
By Thy Passion, O Christ, Thou didst darken the sun, and by the light of Thy Resurrection Thou didst enlighten all things: accept our evening hymn, O Lover of mankind.[^2268]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 3.*

*Then Theotokia Stichera, in the same Tone:*
*Verse:* After her shall virgins be brought unto the King, those near her shall be brought unto Thee.[^2269]

O Church of God prepared for the Godhead of the All-Holy Trinity, O Maiden full of divine joy, river of every goodness proceeding out of paradise beyond understanding: do thou joyfully grant unstinting gifts unto all thy servants, O Pure Lady, that we all may faithfully give thanks unto thee, for thou art the sole mediatress of human nature, O Most Pure One.[^2270]

*Verse:* Hear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.[^2271]

O Heaven of the King of all! O tabernacle that contained God, unhewn mountain, golden urn containing Christ the God-flowing Manna, noetic ark of the New Covenant, O Theotokos, all-blameless and pure! Deliver us from every affliction, and vouchsafe us the inheritance of the righteous.[^2272]

*Verse:* I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.[^2273]

Thou knowest, O Christ, my many sins, and do Thou grant me remission; and for the remainder of my life, strengthen me in Thee to live with godly compunction of heart and inward repentance; and at the dreadful hour may I obtain mercy: standing on the right hand and receiving that which Thou hast prepared for them that have worthily served Thee, O Savior.[^2274]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion, Tone 3:*
A recreation and a second life have we through thee, O most pure Mary, who didst bear the Creator of creation united with human nature in thy womb, Who raised us up from Hades and death, and gave us eternal life, so that we ever cry unto thee, O Virgin: Rejoice, thou who hast united earthly things with the heavenly! Rejoice, hope of all the ends of the earth, and mediatress and protection! Rejoice, thou who by the Resurrection of thy Son hast enlightened all things, and grantest great mercy unto the world![^2275]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart (Luke 2:29). Trisagion, and after Our Father: Resurrectional Troparion. Glory... both now... its Theotokion. Litany: "Have mercy on us, O God," and the Dismissal.*[^2276]

*On Saturday at Great Vespers.*

*At "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing resurrectional stichera.*

*Tone 3.*

By Thy Cross, O Christ Savior, the dominion of death was dissolved, and the delusion of the devil was brought to naught; and the human race, being saved by faith, ever offers a hymn unto Thee.[^2277]

All things were enlightened by Thy Resurrection, O Lord, and Paradise was opened once again; and all creation praising Thee, ever offers a hymn unto Thee.[^2278]

I glorify the power of the Father and of the Son, and I praise the authority of the Holy Spirit: the undivided, uncreated Godhead, the consubstantial Trinity, reigning unto ages of ages.[^2279]

*Other stichera, Anatolian.*
*The same Tone.*
We bow down before Thy precious Cross, O Christ, and we praise and glorify Thy Resurrection; for by Thy wound were we all healed.[^2280]

We praise the Savior incarnate of the Virgin; for for our sake was He crucified, and on the third day He arose, granting us great mercy.[^2281]

Having descended unto them that were in Hades, Christ proclaimed good tidings, saying: Take courage, now have I conquered! I am the Resurrection, I will lead you forth, having destroyed the gates of death![^2282]

Standing unworthily in Thine all-pure house, we send up our evening hymn, crying out of the depths: O Christ God, Who didst enlighten the world by Thy three-day Resurrection, deliver Thy people from the hand of Thine enemies, O Lover of mankind![^2283]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion, Tone 3:*
How shall we not marvel at thy God-manbearing childbearing, O Most Honored...[^2284]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 3.*

*Dogmatic Theotokion (continued):* ...Lady? For having received no trial of man, O All-Blameless One, thou gavest birth without a father unto a Son in the flesh, Who was begotten before the ages of the Father without a mother, Who in no wise underwent change, or confusion, or division, but preserved intact the property of both essences. Therefore, O Mother and Virgin Lady, entreat Him that the souls of them that in orthodox manner confess thee to be the Theotokos may be saved.[^2285]

*Entrance with the censer. O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon and Litanies.*[^2286]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 3:*
By Thy Passion, O Christ, Thou didst darken the sun, and by the light of Thy Resurrection Thou didst enlighten all things: accept our evening hymn, O Lover of mankind.[^2287]

*Other stichera, alphabetical.*
*Verse:* The Lord is King, He is clothed with majesty.[^2288]

Thy life-receiving Resurrection, O Lord, enlightened the whole inhabited world, and called back Thine own corrupted creation. Therefore, having been set free from Adam's curse, we cry aloud: O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee![^2289]

*Verse:* For He hath established the world, which shall not be moved.[^2290]

Being unchangeable God, suffering in the flesh Thou wast changed; and creation, unable to bear the sight, bowed down in fear, and groaning praised Thy long-suffering; and having descended into Hades, on the third day Thou didst arise, granting life unto the world and great mercy.[^2291]

*Verse:* Holiness becomes Thine house, O Lord, unto length of days.[^2292]

That Thou mightest deliver our race from death, O Christ, Thou didst endure death; and having arisen on the third day from the dead, Thou didst raise with Thyself those who acknowledged Thee as God, and didst enlighten the world: O Lord, glory to Thee![^2293]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Without seed from the Holy Spirit, and by the will of the Father, thou didst conceive the Son of God, Who before the ages was of the Father without a mother; and for our sake, having come from thee without a father, thou didst bear Him in the flesh, and nursed the Infant with milk. Therefore cease not to entreat Him, that our souls may be delivered from tribulations.[^2294]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart (Luke 2:29). Trisagion, and after Our Father:*
*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 3:*
Let the heavens be glad, let the earth rejoice; for the Lord hath wrought...[^2295]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 3.*

*Resurrectional Troparion (continued):* ...might with His arm; He hath trampled down death by death; He became the firstborn of the dead; He hath delivered us from the belly of Hades, and hath granted great mercy unto the world.[^2296]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Thee who didst mediate the salvation of our race, do we hymn, O Virgin Theotokos! For in the flesh assumed from thee, Thy Son and our God, having through the Cross accepted the Passion, delivered us from corruption, as the Lover of mankind.[^2297]

*On Saturday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
He Who of old by divine nod gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, He is supremely glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.[^2298]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

As the Mother of God, shown forth by the divine Spirit and by the good pleasure of the Father, do we the faithful crown thee with pure hymns, O all-pure Bride of God, and with the Archangel we kiss thee with songs, unto our salvation.[^2299]

From the ribs of Adam, indeed, was Eve fashioned by the ancient creation of God; but from the womb of the Theotokos Christ appeared, being our God: having become man without change, the Pre-Eternal One became subject to time.[^2300]

*Glory...*
He Who condemned Eve's womb to bring forth children in sorrows and in pain, dwelt in thy womb, O pure Virgin, being our God: He appeared in the flesh beyond understanding, and cancelled the debt of the first mother.[^2301]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Having fallen into the depth of despair by heavy burdens, earnestly do we call unto thee, O Mother of God: Help, O Lady, us who are being drowned by the deeds of deceitful transgressions; for thee alone have we as our hope after God.[^2302]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
Thou Who didst bring all things out of nothingness, which are fashioned by the Word and accomplished by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.[^2303]

The rod of Aaron prefigured thee, which budded without watering, O all-pure Theotokos, who without seed gavest birth unto God incarnate without change.[^2304]

Bearing the divine Fire, as a lamp-stand, O Most Pure Lady...[^2305]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Troparion (continued):* ...the Prophet in the Spirit foresaw thee bearing sweet fragrance unto those in the world, and life eternal.[^2306]

*Glory...*
*Troparion:* Let us approach the Archangel Gabriel, crying aloud unto the Virgin with hymns: Rejoice! for through thee the ancestral curse of condemnation is dissolved.[^2307]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Having thee, O most holy Theotokos, as a wall of salvation, we sinners are saved; despise not, O Lady, and put not to shame our supplications.[^2308]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou hast established steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore we cry unto Thee, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^2309]

*Troparion 1:* The noetic mountain, the shadow of virtues, did Habakkuk of old, seeing thee in godly manner in the Spirit, proclaim, O Most Pure One, as the Word Who came from the south, having received flesh from thee.[^2310]

*Troparion 2:* A great, uncut mountain did Daniel see thee in the Spirit, manifesting the incorrupt purity of thy virginity, O Lady, from which the Stone was cut, Christ the Word, casting down the deceit of idols.[^2311]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* A marvelous and fertile mountain did David foretell thee; for the Only-begotten Son of the Father was well pleased, O Pure One, to dwell in thee, being incarnate; therefore in the spirit we cry unto thee: Rejoice![^2312]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Being all-good and a fervent protectress unto sinners and the humble, O all-pure Lady Mother of God, from tribulations, and afflictions, and sins, save thy servants.[^2313]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* As Isaiah figuratively saw God exalted upon a throne, borne aloft by the Angels of glory, he cried: Woe is me, wretched one! for I have foreseen God incarnate, the Unwaning Light, and ruling over the world in peace.[^2314]

*Troparion 1:* The rod of Jesse hath budded forth an unfading flower, the Virgin Mary, conceiving without seed the unoriginate God from the Divine Spirit and the Father, Who took dominion over the mighty kingdoms of the Gentiles, in Whom the nations have hoped.[^2315]

*Troparion 2:* The Prince of Peace hath come, reigning upon the throne of David, God being incarnate from thee, O Theotokos; O wonder! and having put an end to war, He...[^2316]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...honored the princes of Moab,[^2317] and showed thee forth as Queen who gavest Him birth.

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Manifesting thy spotless beauty, from which Christ clothed Himself in flesh without seed, O Virgin, Isaiah cries aloud, calling: The Lord of glory comes upon a light cloud, and casting away the darkness of delusion, He will enlighten us.[^2318]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Having conceived from the Holy Spirit the Word consubstantial with the Father, O Virgin, thou didst give birth unto Him existing in two natures, perfect God and perfect man, Whose bodily manifestation we honor in faith.[^2319]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch out Thine exalted arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.[^2320]

*Troparion 1:* In songs the most wise one prefigures thee as a bed, O All-Praised One, whereon God rested, being incarnate in hypostasis from thee, and He glorified thee, being born without confusion.[^2321]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast a chosen garment for the Word, O all-praised Virgin; for having received flesh from thee as a divine purple robe, He reigned, having clothed Himself in godly manner.[^2322]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thou wast the receptacle of the divine union, O Bride of God, brighter than gold; for through thee God became as man, and God conversed with men as man.[^2323]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Put to death the heresy of wickedness of those who act impiously against thee, O all-praised Virgin, for they melt away with envy, beholding the most glorious likeness of thine image.[^2324]

*Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3:* From the warmth of faith I cry unto thee, O Theotokos, with unworthy lips and a defiled heart: Save me, who am submerged in sins; show compassion unto me, who am slain by despair, that saved I may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Virgin, the help of Christians![^2325]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The three Children of old bowed not down before the golden image, the Persian object of worship, singing in the midst of the furnace: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^2326]

*Troparion 1:* The burning bush and the flame joined together in conformity, and both appearing unconsumed, clearly manifest thee, O Virgin...[^2327]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 1 (continued):* ...manifest thee; for thou didst give birth unto God, and remainest a virgin.[^2328]

*Troparion 2:* The fleece and dew figuratively shown unto Gideon prefigure thy childbearing; for thou alone dost carry the Divine Word in thy womb like rain, O Virgin Mother.[^2329]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The fire of my sin creates a flame for me worse than Gehenna; quench this, O Pure One, by thy mercy, directing me unto the light through repentance.[^2330]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Venerating the manifestation of thy form, O all-pure Theotokos, we all have thee as the archetype, an unceasing protectress, and a well-pleasing protection unto God.[^2331]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The Babylonian furnace burned not the Children, neither did the fire of the Godhead consume the Virgin; therefore with the Children let us, the faithful, cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^2332]

*Troparion 1:* Having found thee as a most radiant lily in the midst of thorns, shining with the brightness of beauty, the Father was pleased that thou be the unwedded Bride, through the Divine Spirit, as a dwelling for the Son.[^2333]

*Troparion 2:* The most holy Virgin, holier than the hosts on high, all-immaculate, do I manifestly praise without doubting; for thou didst bear in thy womb the Creator of these, Who received flesh from thee in unconfused union.[^2334]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Having preserved unstolen virginity, O Virgin, thou wast truly shown forth as the Mother of the Son of God, having by the Father's good pleasure become a bride, and an incorruptible receptacle of the glory of the Spirit.[^2335]

*Both now... Theotokion:* He Who is by nature immaterial and invisible God, simply and ineffably beyond nature is born man from the holy Virgin, seen as twofold in one hypostasis, wherein He is visible in depictions.[^2336]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* A new and godlike marvel! For the Lord manifestly passes through the shut door of virginity: naked in His entrance, and at His departure God appeared bearing flesh, and the door remains shut. Her do we ineffably magnify as the Mother of God.[^2337]

*Troparion 1:* A holy fruit of a holy root: for the Theotokos, the maiden, budded forth unfading life, bringing to perfection the parents who were barren and sanctified by the Law of God; and Anna rejoices, having received an infant in her old age, the Mother of God, whom we glorify.[^2338]

*Troparion 2:* New and godlike is the birthgiving of thy holy and God-bearing womb...[^2339]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 2 (continued):* ...O Pure One; for in it the form into man was written by the Father's finger, the Son was incarnate with the Holy Spirit; Whom unconfusedly, as God and man, we magnify.[^2340]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Every judgment cries out against me; for the deeds of sinful actions create a heap, threatening me, and the soul knows all wherewith it shall be condemned, and trembles at the flame of Gehenna; from which before the end, deliver me by thy prayers, O Lady.[^2341]

*Both now... Theotokion:* After giving birth thou wast shown forth incorrupt, O Pure One; for unchangeably, O Theotokos, thou didst bear in the flesh as man the Creator of all, beyond nature by the Divine Spirit, not having departed from the Father's essence, and again thou didst remain a Virgin. Therefore glorifying thee, we complete the hymn.[^2342]

*Then: It is truly meet: Trisagion. Resurrectional Hypakoe, and the rest.*[^2343]

*On Sunday Morning, at the Midnight Office.*[^2344]
*Canon to the Most Holy Trinity. (Its acrostic: I praise Thee, O Trinity, the one Sovereignty of God). By Metrophanes.*[^2345]
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:* He Who of old by divine nod gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, He is supremely glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.[^2346]

*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity, our God, glory to Thee.*[^2347]

*Troparion 1:* Incomprehensible one Sovereignty of the Lord, and one Trinitarian Godhead! Vouchsafe me Thy tri-radiant light, that I may sing of Thee, Who art unceasingly hymned with thrice-holy songs by angelic lips.[^2348]

*Troparion 2:* Sacredly all the immaterial ranks sing of Thee, as the creative cause, unique, tri-radiant, manifestly supreme; Whom we, the multitudes of men, also sing with one accord, and faithfully glorify with earthen lips.[^2349]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Mind, and Word, and Spirit do theologians fittingly and significantly call Thee: designating the passionless begetting of the Son from the unbegotten Father, O God, sole Principle of all, together with the procession of the Divine Spirit.[^2350]

*Both now... Theotokion:* As Thou art the Lover of mankind by nature, having assumed human essence, O Word of God, Thou hast shone forth as Trinitarian, the supreme single-principled...[^2351]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 3.*

*Theotokion (continued):* ...Light of the one Godhead, having shown forth unto all as supremely glorified the all-pure Virgin who bore Thee.[^2352]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who didst bring all things out of nothingness, which are fashioned by the Word and accomplished by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.[^2353]

*Troparion 1:* Elijah of old, having commanded to pour water thrice upon the pieces of wood, figuratively revealed the Trinitarian hypostasis of the unique Sovereignty of God.[^2354]

*Troparion 2:* The corruptible nature of earthborn mortals hymns Thee, the one and tri-radiant immutable Creator, and cries unto Thee: O Master, deliver me from all-diverse change, and save me.[^2355]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Evenly uttering words of the Prophets, and of the glorious Apostles and preachers of the faith, we the faithful glorify Thee, the equally operating Trinity, O God of all.[^2356]

*Both now... Theotokion:* From the high throne Christ descended, exalting man as the Lover of mankind, through thee, O Most Pure One, and the tri-solar light shone forth upon all.[^2357]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3, Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):* O undivided and inseparable Trinity! Have mercy upon us who worship Thee, and hymn Thee piously, and unceasingly cry unto Thee: Save those whom Thou hast gathered for the sake of the mercy of Thy tender compassion, in Thy holy church, to glorify Thy dominion; for as Thou art merciful, Thou knowest the weakness of our heart.[^2358]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* I am wholly changed by my lack of self-control, and in my senselessness by my lack of fear, feeling nothing, wretch that I am; I tremble not at the day at which all things visible and invisible tremble. O Lady, help me, and dispel the perplexity of my heart; for thou thyself art the hope and protectress of thy servants.[^2359]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou hast established steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore we cry unto Thee, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^2360]

*Troparion 1:* As a twofold branch from the Father, as from a root, sprouted the Son and the Upright Spirit, consubstantial sproutings, God-planted, and...[^2361]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 1 (continued):* ...co-unoriginate flowers: so that there are three lights of the Godhead. *(Twice)*[^2362]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* The multitudes of noetic beings unceasingly hymn Thee, the incomprehensible God; with whom we also glorify Thee, saying: O super-essential Trinity, save Thy servants, as the Lover of mankind.[^2363]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thou hast inflamed us with Thy love, O most merciful Word of God, Who for our sake wast incarnate without change, and mystically didst teach the one tri-radiant Godhead; therefore we glorify Thee.[^2364]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* As Isaiah figuratively saw God exalted upon a throne, borne aloft by the Angels of glory, he cried: Woe is me, wretched one! for I have foreseen God incarnate, the Unwaning Light, and ruling over the world in peace.[^2365]

*Troparion 1:* As Isaiah figuratively saw the one Sovereign Lord, God, glorified in three Persons with pure voices, a seraph was sent forthwith to proclaim the tri-radiant Essence and the tri-solar Monad. *(Twice)*[^2366]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* O tri-solar Monad, who of old didst bring into being out of nothingness the nature of all things visible and invisible: having delivered from all temptations those who faithfully hymn Thee as the one God, vouchsafe them Thy glory.[^2367]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Having become the radiant and pure bridal chamber of God, O Virgin, we hymn thee in love and call thee blessed; for from thee was born Christ in twofold essences and wills, Who is One of the Trinity and the Lord of glory.[^2368]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* The lowest abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch out Thine exalted arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.[^2369]

*Troparion 1:* Being a sojourner, Abraham was vouchsafed figuratively to receive the unique Lord in three hypostases, super-essential, yet in human forms. *(Twice)*[^2370]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* Direct the hearts of Thy servants unto the unapproachable light, O tri-solar Lord! and grant the radiance of Thy glory unto our souls, that they may be shone upon by Thine ineffable beauty...[^2371]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 3.*

*Both now... Theotokion:* Open unto me the doors of light, of Him Who was born of thy womb, O Most Pure One, that I may behold the tri-radiant ray of the Godhead, and glorify thee, the all-radiant Lady.[^2372]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3, Podoben: "Today the Virgin" (Дѣ́ва дне́сь):* When Thou comest upon earth, O Master, to judge all whom Thou didst fashion from the earth according to Thine image, show compassion as Thou art merciful, and deliver all from Thy righteous judgment; and send us not into the fire of Gehenna, as Thou alone art Christ, the abundantly merciful.[^2373]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Grant me sighing, O Virgin, even as unto the publican, and tears as unto the harlot, that offering these unto thee, I may cry: Have mercy upon me, O all-merciful one, and destroy my perplexity by thy grace, bestowing upon me a cloud of compunction; for unto thee have I fled, O Most Pure One.[^2374]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* As of old Thou didst bedew the three pious Children in the Chaldean flame with the radiant fire of the Godhead: so also enlighten us who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2375]

*Troparion 1:* Show me forth as a temple of Thy tri-radiant Godhead, O Master, wholly bright, above the cruel darkening of sin and passions, by Thy light-bestowing radiances, crying: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou![^2376]

*Troparion 2:* We proclaim one appearance of the Godhead in three hypostatic and distinct properties: Father, Son, and Spirit, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2377]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* God in three Hypostases appeared unto Abraham of old at the oak of Mamre, rewarding his hospitality with the mercy of Isaac; Whom even now we glorify as the God of our fathers.[^2378]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The Creator of all appeared upon earth, having become man in godly manner from thy virginal and most pure womb, and deified us, O blessed, all-pure, and most clean Theotokos.[^2379]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* United with the unbearable fire, the youth champions of piety, being unharmed by the flame, sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^2380]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 1:* From the unoriginate Father as from a root, the Word and the Spirit, being co-unoriginate, as shoots of the super-essential Godhead, have shown forth the one glory and power of the Trinity: which all we faithful hymn unto the ages. *(Twice)*[^2381]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* By Thy brightnesses Thou dost guide the heavenly ranks, that they may unceasingly hymn with divine thrice-holy songs, O Father, conformed Word, and Spirit, the tri-radiant and equally powerful dominion. Therefore we hymn Thee unto all ages.[^2382]

*Both now... Theotokion:* The prophetic predictions, seeing thy birthgiving from afar, O Theotokos, praised Him Who was born without seed and beyond nature, O Lady! And we also sing of Him with one accord as the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^2383]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* A new and godlike marvel! For the Lord manifestly passes through the shut door of virginity: naked in His entrance, and at His departure God appeared bearing flesh, and the door remains shut. Her do we ineffably magnify as the Mother of God.[^2384]

*Troparion 1:* To behold the tri-radiant glory, the godlike ranks of the incorporeal hosts manifestly desire to ascend on wings on high; yet they stand in deep awe of the unapproachable light, and unceasingly cry out hymns. With them, with one accord, O unique Trinity, we glorify Thee. *(Twice)*[^2385]

*Troparion 2:* With unswerving love, we also upon earth, having received from Thee a noetic and rational soul, hymn Thee, O Master, God of all, the unique essence in truth, and Trinitarian in Persons, from the whole heart. Therefore, O Compassionate One, as Thou art abundantly merciful, show compassion unto us.[^2386]

*Both now... Theotokion:* Show me forth as a radiant temple of Thy single-principled and tri-radiant Godhead, purely to serve Thee, the Creator of all, and noetically to behold Thine ineffable glory, through the prayers of the only Theotokos, whom we worthily magnify as most glorious.[^2387]

*Then, the Trinitarian Hymns of Gregory of Sinai: It is truly meet in godly manner. Then: More honorable than the Cherubim. Trisagion. After Our Father, the troparia: Have mercy on us, O Lord. Then, the litany, and the Dismissal.*[^2388]

*On Sunday at Matins,*[^2389]
*After the Six Psalms, we sing: "God is the Lord, and hath appeared unto us" in Tone 3.*[^2390]
*Resurrectional Troparion: "Let the heavens be glad..." Glory... both now... Theotokion: "Thee who didst mediate the salvation..."*[^2391]
*Then, the usual Kathismata of the Psalter.*[^2392]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*After the 1st Kathisma, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*
Christ is risen from the dead, the firstfruits of them that slept, the Firstborn of creation, and Maker of all things that have come to be; He hath renewed in Himself the corrupted nature of our race. No longer, O death, dost thou hold dominion; for the Master of all hath destroyed thy power.[^2393]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*[^2394]

Terrified at Thine unchangeable Godhead and Thy voluntary passion, O Lord, Hades lamented within itself: I tremble at the bodily hypostasis of incorruption; I behold the Invisible One, secretly waging war against me; therefore they also whom I hold captive cry out: Glory, O Christ, to Thy Resurrection![^2395]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Heavenly beings rejoiced in love, and earthly beings trembled in awe, when the all-pure voice came upon thee, O Theotokos; for one solemnity shone forth for both, when the incorporeal one brought joy unto thee. Therefore with the Angel we cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Pure One, dwelling-place of God![^2396]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 3, Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*
Having tasted death in the flesh, O Lord, Thou hast cut off the bitterness of death by Thy rising, and hast strengthened man against it, revoking the prevailing power of the ancestral curse. O Defender of our life, Lord, glory to Thee![^2397]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*[^2398]

The incomprehensible mystery of Thy Crucifixion and the ineffable wonder of Thy Rising do we the faithful proclaim, an unspoken mystery; for today death and Hades have been despoiled, and the human race is clothed in incorruption. Therefore giving thanks we cry unto Thee: Glory, O Christ, to Thy Rising![^2399]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
The incomprehensible and uncircumscribable One, consubstantial with the Father and the Spirit, didst thou mystically contain in thy womb, O Theotokos; one and unconfused Godhead of the Trinity have we known, to glorify thy childbearing in the world. Therefore thankfully we cry unto thee: Rejoice, O full of grace![^2400]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*After the Amomos, the Hypakoe, Tone 3:*[^2401]
Astonishing by his appearance and bedewing by his words, the radiant Angel said unto the myrrh-bearing women: Why seek ye the Living One in the tomb? He is risen, Who emptied the graves! Understand the Incorruptible Changer of corruption. Say unto God: How awesome are Thy works! for Thou hast saved the human race.[^2402]

*Songs of Ascents, Tone 3:*[^2403]
*Antiphon 1. We sing, repeating:*
Thou hast led captive Zion out of Babylon; draw me also, O Word, away from passions unto life.[^2404]

They that sow in the south with divine tears shall reap the sheaves of everlasting life in joy.[^2405]

*Glory...*
Unto the Holy Spirit is all thanksgiving, even as He co-radiates with the Father and the Son, in Whom all things live and move. *Both now... the same.*[^2406]

*Antiphon 2:*
Except the Lord build the house of virtues, in vain do we labor; and while He protects the soul, no one shall destroy our city.[^2407]

By the Spirit the saints are ever unto Thee, O Christ, even as unto the Father, the fruit of the womb adopted as sons.[^2408]

*Glory...*
Through the Holy Spirit is every holiness and wisdom foreseen; for He gives essence unto every creature. Unto Him let us minister; for He is God, even as unto the Father and the Word. *Both now... the same.*[^2409]

*Antiphon 3:*
Blessed are they that fear the Lord, walking in the ways of His commandments; for they shall eat the life-bearing fruit of all virtues.[^2410]

Around Thy table, O Chief Shepherd, be glad, beholding Thine offspring bearing branches of well-doing.[^2411]

*Glory...*
Through the Holy Spirit is all wealth of glory, from Whom is grace and life unto every creature; for He is hymned with the Father and with the Word. *Both now... the same.*[^2412]

*Prokeimenon, Tone 3:*
Say among the heathen that the Lord is King; for He hath established the world, which shall not be moved.[^2413]
*Verse: Sing unto the Lord a new song:*[^2414]
Let every breath praise the Lord.[^2415]
*Verse: Praise God in His saints:*[^2416]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*The Resurrection Gospel, and the rest in order.*[^2417]
*Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ... Psalm 50.*[^2418]

*Resurrection Canon.*[^2419]
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
He Who of old by divine gesture gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel, He is our God, most glorious is He; unto Him alone do we sing, for He hath been glorified.[^2420]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*[^2421]
*Troparion 1:*
He Who condemned the earth to bring forth thorns as fruit unto the transgressor through sweat, He is our God, Who received in the flesh a crown of thorns from a lawless hand, and hath undone the curse; for He hath been glorified.[^2422]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
A conqueror and vanquisher of death was He shown forth, Who seemed to fear death; for having assumed an animate, passible flesh, He is our God, and having fought against the tyrant, He raised all with Himself; for He hath been glorified.[^2423]

*Theotokion:*
All nations glorify thee as the true Theotokos, who without seed gavest birth; for having descended into thy sanctified womb, He is our God, Who was substantialized according to our nature, and God and man was born of thee.[^2424]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*[^2425]
*(Whose acrostic is: I sing a third hymn unto Thee, O Word of God.)*[^2426]
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
Let us sing a new song, O ye peoples, unto Him Who was born of the Virgin for our salvation, and made earthly things one with the heavenly; for He hath been glorified.[^2427]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
The human race, enslaved by the sin-loving tyrant, Christ redeemed by divine blood, and having divinized it, hath renewed it; for He hath been glorified.[^2428]

*Troparion 2:*
As mortal indeed, willing death, did Christ, the treasury of life, taste of it; and being immortal by nature, He hath made the dead alive; for He hath been glorified.[^2429]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^2430]
*(Whose acrostic is: I sing a third hymn unto thee, O Theotokos.)*[^2431]
*Ode 1. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^2432]
*Troparion 1:*
Every nature of heavenly beings bends the knee, O Virgin, unto Him Who was incarnate of thee, and with earthly beings, fittingly those beneath the earth; for He hath been glorified.[^2433]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 2:*
O the reconciliations that are in thee! For the Bestower of good things without envy, as God, Who bestowed the divine Spirit upon us, assumed flesh from thee, O Maiden; for He hath been glorified.[^2434]

*Katavasia: I shall open my mouth:*[^2435]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
O Almighty Master, Who broughtest all things from non-being into being, fashioned by the Word, perfected by the Spirit: establish me in Thy love.[^2436]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
The impious one was put to shame by Thy Cross; for he made a pit, which having dug, he fell into it; but the horn of the humble, O Christ, was exalted in Thy Resurrection.[^2437]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
The proclamation of piety to the nations covered the seas like water, O Lover of mankind! For having risen from the tomb, Thou didst reveal the light of the Trinity.[^2438]

*Theotokion:*
Glorious things were spoken of thee, O animate city of Him Who reigns for ever; for through thee, O Lady, God lived together with those upon earth.[^2439]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
Thou hast broken the bow of the enemy, and crushed the shields by Thy strength, O Master Christ, our confirmation. Holy art Thou, O Lord.[^2440]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
A purification from the defilements of idols hast thou been shown forth, O most precious Cross; for Jesus the super-divine stretched out His hands upon thee.[^2441]

*Troparion 2:*
That all we faithful may worship thee, O life-receiving tomb: in thee was buried and arose Christ, truly our God.[^2442]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
A rod from the root of Jesse, the Virgin, having prophetically blossomed, shone forth Thee as a flower unto us, O Christ. Holy art Thou, O Lord.[^2443]

*Troparion 2:*
That Thou mightest make the earthborn partakers of divinity, Thou, having impoverished Thyself, didst assume our flesh from the Virgin. Holy art Thou, O Lord.[^2444]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
Thou hast established a steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thine only-begotten Son Thou gavest over unto death for us. Therefore we cry unto Thee, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^2445]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Wounds and bruises, O Christ, Thou didst mercifully endure, suffering the insult of blows upon the cheeks, and patiently bearing spittings, through which Thou hast wrought salvation for me. Glory to Thy power, O Lord![^2446]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
With a mortal body, O Life, Thou didst partake of death, because of the suffering of the poor and the sighing of Thy needy; and having corrupted the corrupter, O Most Glorious One, Thou hast raised all with Thyself; for He hath been glorified.[^2447]

*Theotokion:*
Remember, O Christ, the flock which Thou hast acquired by Thy passion, having accepted the merciful entreaties of Thy most glorious Mother; and having visited the afflicted, deliver it by Thy power, O Lord.[^2448]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
Having heard the strange and ineffable mystery of Thy nativity, I feared, and rejoicing I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind![^2449]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Having fashioned man according to Thine image, O Lover of mankind, when he was slain by the sin of transgression, Thou, being crucified at the Place of the Skull, didst save him.[^2450]

*Troparion 2:*
The dead indeed whom death had swallowed up, it gave back; and the corrupting realm of Hades was destroyed when Thou didst arise from the tomb, O Lord.[^2451]

*Theotokion:*
O Mary pure, golden censer, without confusion as One of the Trinity God the Word, having descended into thee and become incarnate, filled the world with sweet fragrance.[^2452]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
O Master Who didst set the mountains by the measure of divine wisdom, Thou art the stone hewn from the Virgin without hands. Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind![^2453]

*Troparion 2:*
Thou hast healed our ailing nature, O Master, having united it entirely, most afflicted from the Virgin, unto Thine all-pure divinity, O Word.[^2454]

*Troparion 3:*
Thou art my portion, O Lord, and desired inheritance, Who hast united me from the Virgin, O Word, unto Thy hypostasis, having become a hypostasis in the flesh.[^2455]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
Unto Thee do I rise early, the Creator of all, Who surpassest every mind in peace; for Thy commandments are light, wherein do Thou guide me.[^2456]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Being betrayed unto an unjust judge through Jewish envy, O All-seeing One, Who judgest all the earth righteously, Thou didst deliver ancient Adam from condemnation.[^2457]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
Thy peace grant unto Thy churches, O Christ, through the invincible power of Thy Cross, O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, and save our souls.[^2458]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Theotokion:*
A holy tabernacle and more spacious than the heavens, as having received the Word of God Whom all creation cannot contain, thou alone hast been shown forth, O Ever-Virgin.[^2459]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
Upon earth Thou wast seen, the Invisible One, and willingly didst live together with men, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early unto Thee, we praise Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^2460]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Being pierced with a spear in Thy side, O my Christ, Thou hast released from the curse her that was created from man's rib, who became the mediator of destruction unto all men.[^2461]

*Troparion 2:*
Being equal in essence unto the Father, Thou didst raise from the dead the sacred temple of Thine all-pure and all-honorable body, O Christ our Savior.[^2462]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
O Word of God, Thy Son, O Virgin, the Maker of Adam the first-formed, is not a creature, though He created for Himself an animate flesh from thee.[^2463]

*Troparion 2:*
Equal to the Father, Thy Son, O Virgin, the Word of God, is a perfect Hypostasis in two natures, Jesus the Lord, perfect God and man.[^2464]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
The lowest abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch out Thine exalted arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.[^2465]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
An abyss of mercy and compassions hath compassed me by Thy tender-hearted condescension; for having become incarnate, O Master, and having come in the form of a servant, Thou didst divinize me and glorify me together with Thyself.[^2466]

*Troparion 2:*
The slayer of men received slaying, beholding the slain one brought to life; these are the figures of Thy Resurrection, O Christ, and the victorious trophies of Thine all-pure passion.[^2467]

*Theotokion:*
O All-Pure One, who alone beyond understanding art mediator between the Creator and mankind, pray that thy Son may be merciful and compliant unto thy servants who have sinned.[^2468]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
Jonah, having become a natural figure of the habitation that is in the nethermost Hades, cried aloud: Bring up my life from corruption, O Lover of mankind![^2469]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
By wounds Wast Thou tried, and those wounded by Hades Thou didst raise together with Thyself through the passion of the Cross; therefore I cry: Bring up my life from corruption, O Lover of mankind![^2470]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 2:*
The gates of Hades are opened unto Thee with fear, O Christ, and the vessels of the enemy are despoiled. Therefore the women met Thee, having received joy instead of sorrow.[^2471]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
He Who is according to our nature is depicted from the incorrupt Virgin: He Who is indivisible in form, having become a man in form and substance, yet without changing in His divinity.[^2472]

*Troparion 2:*
Deliver me from the abysses of sins and the storm of passions, O All-Pure One; for thou art a haven and an abyss of wonders unto them that flee unto thee with faith.[^2473]

*Kontakion, Tone 3, Podoben: "Today the Virgin" (Дѣ́ва дне́сь):*
Thou didst rise today from the tomb, O Compassionate One, and didst lead us up from the gates of death; today Adam dances and Eve rejoices, and together the prophets with the patriarchs unceasingly praise the divine dominion of Thy power.[^2474]

*Ikos:*
Let heaven and earth dance today, and praise Christ God with one mind, for He hath raised the prisoners from the graves. All creation rejoices together, offering worthy hymns to the Creator of all and our Redeemer; for having led men up from Hades today as the Giver of life, He exalts them together into the heavens, and casts down the haughtiness of the enemy, and smashes the gates of Hades by the divine dominion of His power.[^2475]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
As of old Thou didst bedew the three pious children in the Chaldean flame with the radiant fire of divinity, so also enlighten us who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2476]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
The bright veil of the temple was rent in the crucifixion of the Maker, revealing unto the faithful the truth hidden in the Scripture, who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2477]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
By Thy pierced side, with drops of God-flowing, life-creating blood, O Christ, dispensational dripping upon the earth, Thou hast remade them that are of the earth, who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2478]

*Glory... Trinitarian:*
Let us praise the good Spirit together with the Father, and with the Only-begotten Son, as faithful honoring one Principle in three, and one *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

Godhead, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^2479]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
The proud tyrant became the plaything of children; for having trampled underfoot the sevenfold flame as dust, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.[^2480]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Beholding not a mere man hanging upon the Cross, but God incarnate, the sun is darkened; unto Whom we also sing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.[^2481]

*Troparion 2:*
Fearful Hades, having received the One Who is mighty in divinity, the Giver of incorruption, cast out the souls of the righteous, who cried: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.[^2482]

*Theotokion:*
A costly treasure, O blessed one, hast thou been shown forth, O All-Pure One, unto them that confess thee with a pure heart as Mother of God; for from thee was incarnate the God of our fathers.[^2483]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
The Lord of glory, Who holds the heavenly powers, Who sits with the Father, being borne by virginal hands: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.[^2484]

*Troparion 2:*
Fierce was death; but having become God-hypostatic flesh from the Virgin, Thou hast destroyed this death that mingled itself with Thee: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.[^2485]

*Troparion 3:*
We all have known thee as Theotokos, who gavest birth to God; for thou hast borne One of the Trinity Who was incarnate of thee. Blessed, O All-Pure One, is the Fruit of thy womb.[^2486]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
United with the unbearable fire, the youths, champions of piety, unharmed by the flame, sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages![^2487]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
The brightness of the temple was rent when Thy Cross was planted on the Place of the Skull, and creation bowed down in fear, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^2488]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
Thou didst rise, O Christ, from the tomb, and him that fell through deception Thou didst set upright by the Tree through divine power, crying and saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^2489]

*Theotokion:*
A temple of God hast thou been shown forth, an animate receptacle, and an ark; for the Creator unto men- *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

kind hast thou reconciled, O all-pure Mother of God; and worthily, all we works praise thee, and supremely exalt thee unto all ages.[^2490]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
The God-beholding children quenched the flame of material fire with the immaterial, and sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^2491]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
For the impassible Word, while impassible indeed in His divinity, yet as God suffers in the flesh, unto Whom we also sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^2492]

*Troparion 2:*
Having fallen asleep indeed as mortal, Thou didst rise as immortal, O Savior, and savest from death them that sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^2493]

*Glory... Trinitarian:*
Let us piously serve the tri-hypostatic Godhead, ineffably united, and let us sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^2494]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Both now... Theotokion:*
The noetic ranks hast thou surpassed, as Mother, and art near unto God. We bless, O blessed Virgin, thy Childbearing, and supremely exalt it unto all ages.[^2495]

*Troparion 2:*
Natural beauty hast thou shown forth most fair, flashing forth the flesh of the Godhead. We bless, O blessed Virgin, thy Childbearing, and supremely exalt it unto all ages.[^2496]

*Then: "My soul magnifies the Lord," with the refrain: "More honorable than the Cherubim."*[^2497]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
A new and godlike marvel! For the Lord manifestly passes through the shut door of virginity: naked in His entrance, and at His departure God appeared bearing flesh, and the door remains shut. Her do we ineffably magnify as the Mother of God.[^2498]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
It is awesome to behold Thee, the Creator, lifted up upon the Tree, O Word of God, and as God suffering in the flesh for Thy servants, and lying breathless in the tomb, yet releasing the dead from Hades; therefore Thee, O Christ, as almighty do we magnify.[^2499]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 2:*
From mortal corruption hast Thou saved the forefathers, O Christ, having been laid dead in the tomb, and Thou didst blossom forth life, having raised the dead, guiding human nature unto the light, and clothing it with divine incorruption. There- *On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

fore as the Fountain of ever-living light do we magnify Thee.[^2500]

*Theotokion:*
A receptacle of light did He show thee forth, O Pure One, when God was ineffably and unchangeably incarnate of thee, having lifted up Adam's substance upon the Cross: having become the Firstborn from the dead, God abides in the highest, together with the Father and the divine Spirit glorified.[^2501]

*Another Canon. Heirmos:*
Wounded by the sweet arrow of thine all-pure childbearing, O Pure One, marveling at thy most desirable beauty, with angelic hymns do we worthily magnify thee as the Mother of God.[^2502]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*
*Troparion 1:*
Honor for all men hast Thou poured forth from dishonorable death; having tasted of it by Thy crucifixion, O Savior, in Thy mortal substance, Thou hast bestowed incorruption upon me, O Christ, as the Lover of mankind.[^2503]

*Troparion 2:*
Thou hast saved me, having risen from the tomb, O Christ, and didst ascend and bring me unto Thy Father and Begetter, and didst seat me together at His right hand, through the tender-heartedness of Thy mercy, O Lord.[^2504]

*Another Canon. The same Heirmos:*
*Refrain: Most holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:*
Satiety of thy praises, O Virgin, can never come unto the pious faithful; for receiving desire upon divine and spiritual desire, they magnify thee as the Mother of God.[^2505]

*Troparion 2:*
Thou hast set for us an unashamed intercessor, her that bare Thee, O Christ. By her entreaties Thou mercifully bestowest upon us the Spirit, the Giver of goodness, Who through Thee proceeds from the Father.[^2506]

*After the Katavasia, the litany. Then: "Holy is the Lord our God." And the Exapostilarion.*[^2507]

*At the Praises*[^2508]
*Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 3:*
Come, all ye nations, understand the power of the awesome mystery; for Christ our Savior, the Word in the beginning, was crucified for us, and was willingly buried, and arose from the dead to save all things. Let us worship Him.[^2509]

Thy guards told of all the wonders, O Lord; but the council of vanity filled their right hand with a bribe, thinking to hide Thy Resurrection, which the world glorifies. Have mercy upon us.[^2510]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

All things were filled with joy, having received the proof of the Resurrection; for Mary Magdalene, having come to the tomb, found an Angel sitting upon the stone, shining in his raiment and saying: Why seek ye the Living One with the dead? He is not here, but is risen, as He said, going before you into Galilee.[^2511]

In Thy light, O Master, we shall see light, O Lover of mankind; for Thou art risen from the dead, granting salvation unto the human race, that all creation may glorify Thee, the only sinless One. Have mercy upon us.[^2512]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian, in the same Tone:*[^2513]
The myrrh-bearing women brought a morning song unto Thee with tears, O Lord; for having sweet spices of fragrant ointment, they reached Thy tomb, striving to anoint Thine all-pure body. But the Angel sitting upon the stone announced good tidings unto them: Why seek ye the Living One with the dead? For having trampled down death, He is risen as God, granting great mercy unto all.[^2514]

The Angel shining upon Thy life-giving tomb said unto the myrrh-bearing women: The Redeemer Who emptied the graves hath despoiled Hades, and is risen on the third day, as the only God and Almighty.[^2515]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*[^2516]
Mary Magdalene sought Thee in the tomb, having come on the first day of the week; and not finding Thee, she lamented, crying out with weeping: Woe is me, O my Savior! Thou hast been stolen away, O King of all. But a pair of life-bearing Angels within the tomb cried aloud: Why weepest thou, O woman? I weep, she says, because they have taken my Lord from the tomb, and I know not where they have laid Him. And turning back, when she beheld Thee, she immediately cried out: My Lord and my God, glory to Thee![^2517]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*[^2518]
The Jews shut up Life in the tomb, but the thief opened paradise with his tongue, calling out and saying: He that was crucified with me and for my sake, hung with me upon the Tree, and appeared unto me sitting upon the throne with the Father; for He is Christ our God, Who hath great mercy.[^2519]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Glory... The Gospel Sticheron.*[^2520]
*Both now... Theotokion:*
*Most blessed art thou, O Virgin Theotokos...*[^2521]
*The Great Doxology. After the Doxology, the troparion: "Today salvation is come to the world..."*[^2522]
*Litanies and the Dismissal.*[^2523]

*At the Liturgy*[^2524]
*Beatitudes, Tone 3:*
When the forefather Adam cast aside Thy commandment, O Christ, Thou didst drive him out of paradise; but the thief, O Compassionate One, who confessed Thee on the Cross, Thou didst settle therein, as he cried: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy kingdom.[^2525]

When Thou wast crucified, O Christ, all creation was visibly transformed, beholding its Master in human nature. Therefore with the thief it cried unto Thee: Remember me when Thou comest in Thy kingdom.[^2526]

Having sinned, Thou didst condemn us by the curse of death, O Giver of life and Lord; but having suffered in Thy body, O sinless Master, Thou hast quickened the dead, who cry: Remember us also in Thy kingdom.[^2527]

Having risen from the dead, Thou hast raised us with Thyself from passions by Thy Resurrection, O Lord, and hast destroyed all the power of death, O Savior. Therefore in faith we cry unto Thee: Remember us also in Thy kingdom.[^2528]

Unto the myrrh-bearing women Thou didst first appear, having risen from the dead, O Savior, crying out: Rejoice! And through them Thou didst announce Thy rising unto Thy friends, O Christ. Therefore in faith we cry unto Thee: Remember us also in Thy kingdom.[^2529]

Upon the mountain Moses stretched out his hands, prefiguring the Cross, and defeated Amalek; and we, having received it by faith as a mighty weapon against demons, all cry: Remember us also in Thy kingdom.[^2530]

*Glory... Trinitarian:*
Let us the faithful worship the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit: the uncreated Trinity in three Persons, and in one Godhead, ever faithfully glorified by the bodiless powers in three sanctifications.[^2531]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Rejoice, O gate of God, through which the incarnate Creator passed, keeping thee sealed. Rejoice, light cloud, bearing Christ, the divine rain. Rejoice, ladder, and heavenly throne. Rejoice, honorable, fertile, and unhewn mountain of God.[^2532]

*On Sunday Evening, Tone 3.*

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^2533]

*On Sunday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 3.*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Great is the power of Thy martyrs" (Ве́лїа мч҃никъ твои́хъ):*[^2534]

*Sticheron 1:* As I sin greatly, and grieve Thee, the Good Master, Who art merciful by nature: receive me returning like the prodigal son, and make me as one of Thy hired servants, O Heavenly Father.[^2535]

*Sticheron 2:* Through slothfulness have I passed the measure of life, and already have I, wretched one, drawn near unto the end; I consider not the judgment awaiting me, nor falling away from God; but turn me, O Savior, and snatch me from all these.[^2536]

*Sticheron 3:* From Gehenna and gnashing of teeth deliver my humble soul, O Lord, and from every other eternal torment, O All-good One, that with faith I may praise Thee, the merciful God, and Lover of mankind by nature.[^2537]

*Then the Stichera in the Menaion to the Saint.*
*Glory: To the Saint. Both now: Theotokion.*
*But if there be no Menaion, we sing these Stichera to the Holy Bodiless Angels:*[^2538]
*Tone the same.*

*Sticheron 1 to the Bodiless Powers:* The choirs of the Bodiless hast Thou established, O Lord, showing forth unto all the wealth of Thy goodness; and Thou hast brought them from non-existence into being, who now praise Thy glory which transcends the mind, with unceasing voices.[^2539]

*Sticheron 2 to the Bodiless Powers:* Great is the power of Thine Angels, O Christ! For being incorporeal, they traverse the world, preserving the churches by the power which is from Thee, O Master, and they pray unto Thee for the inhabited world.[^2540]

*Sticheron 3 to the Bodiless Powers:* Beholding Thy unique, three-sun beauty, the powers that serve Thee show themselves as secondary splendors unto those who are in the world, and shining abundantly, they impart unto us divine brightness.[^2541]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O All-pure One who rulest over all creatures, free me who am fiercely held in sway by the passions of my mind, by thy fervent intercession and maternal prayer, and make me subject unto thy Son and God.[^2542]

*Then: "O Gladsome Radiance." The Prokeimenon of the day. "Vouchsafe, O Lord."*[^2543]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 3:*[^2544]
An evening song do we offer unto Thee, O Christ, with incense and *On Sunday Evening, Tone 3.*

spiritual songs: have mercy and save our souls.[^2545]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, who dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^2546]

Save me, O Lord my God, for Thou art salvation unto all! The tempest of passions troubles me, and the burden of mine iniquities drowns me; stretch forth unto me a hand of help, and lead me up unto the light of compunction, as the only compassionate One and Lover of mankind.[^2547]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^2548]

Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord! For it was planted in a place, and works throughout the world; and it hath shown forth fishermen as Apostles, and Gentiles as martyrs, that they might pray for our souls.[^2549]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O Theotokos, protectress of all who pray unto thee! In thee do we take courage, and in thee do we boast, and in thee is all our hope; pray unto Him Who was born of thee, for Thine unprofitable servants.[^2550]

*Then: "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart." Trisagion, and after "Our Father": troparion and the Dismissal.*[^2551]

*On Sunday at Compline,*
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^2552]
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by His divine bidding gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, most glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.*[^2553]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^2554]

*Troparion 1:* O greatly renowned and only all-holy Virgin Mary, more glorious than the Cherubim, daughter of David, and beauty of Jacob, Mother of our God! Greatly hath thy glory been magnified, and we all glorify thee with faith.[^2555]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast borne in thy hands as a man Him Who by His word stretched out the heaven as a curtain; therefore pray Him unceasingly, O Pure One, to deliver my soul from irrational passions and from every affliction.[^2556]

*Glory:*
O Virgin foreordained before all ages, all-pure purple robe of the King of glory, wherewith He clothed Himself in the latter times: cover the garments of skin of Adam our forefather, and cover me now.[^2557]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Both now:*
Having given birth unto Christ, the unendurable Fire, O Virgin, thou didst dry up the corruption-working sin of old for the human race; therefore dry up the torrents of the passions of my soul by thy prayers.[^2558]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Master, Who didst bring all things into being from non-existence, created by the Word, perfected by the Spirit: establish me in Thy love.*[^2559]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Thy Son, O Pure One, was named Master and Lord with fiery dominion; therefore deliver me, O Lady, from the service of passions by thy prayers.[^2560]

*Troparion 2:* From the tempest of carnal passions swiftly deliver me, thy servant, O All-pure One, who didst bear the calm of salvation and the fountain of dispassion.[^2561]

*Glory:*
Having become a radiant lamp of the never-setting Light, O All-pure One, thou hast illumined the world; therefore by thy rays dispel the cloud of my soul.[^2562]

*Both now:*
Quicken me who am dead in soul from the multitude of evil passions, O Lady, who ineffably didst bear Christ, the Giver of life.[^2563]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast set toward us a steadfast love, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine only-begotten Son unto death for our sake. Therefore we cry unto Thee in thanksgiving: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^2564]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* The warfare of carnal mindedness hath come upon me, and leads my soul captive; but do thou who didst bear the peace surpassing all mind, grant me thy peace, O Lady.[^2565]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast poured forth an intellectual myrrh unto the world from thy womb; therefore free my soul from the defilements of passions, and grant me the myrrh of dispassion, O All-pure One, by thy prayers.[^2566]

*Glory:*
Knowing thee to be the golden urn that manifestly bore the divine-human Christ, the Manna of life, I pray thee: feed my soul that pins away with hunger, and give it to drink of the torrent of the Spirit.[^2567]

*Both now:*
Eve indeed of old introduced death through lack of restraint; but thou, through pure virginity, hast introduced the true life. Therefore deliver me from the death of sin, by thy prayers.[^2568]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Upon earth, O Invisible One, Thou wast seen, and with men Thou didst willingly dwell, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early at dawn unto Thee, we praise Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^2569]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Thy Son, having become redemption for us, O Virgin, delivered all things from corruption; therefore deliver me from carnal mindedness and the assault of passions.[^2570]

*Troparion 2:* With the fire of thy prayers, O Theotokos, burn up all the thorns of passionate thoughts, O thou who didst purify man with the tongs of the coal.[^2571]

*Glory:*
Thou art God's Heifer, who didst bear for our sake the divine Calf, slaughtered out of love for mankind; with His divine blood cleanse my heart.[^2572]

*Both now:*
Formerly I acquired a fair garment of virtues, of which I have stripped myself by my slothfulness. But do thou, O Virgin, clothe me now in a brighter one, by thy prayers.[^2573]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The uttermost abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch forth Thy high arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.*[^2574]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* The fierce crafts of wickedness have shaken the temple of my soul; but do thou who didst bear Christ Who established the earth upon the waters, establish it by thy prayers.[^2575]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast loosed the debt of the earthborn, having given birth unto the Pearl of great price; therefore swiftly loose, O All-pure One, the fetters of my transgressions, and passions, and afflictions.[^2576]

*Glory:*
He that was born of thy womb, the All-good God, hath given thee unto the faithful as a refuge and divine protection. Therefore, O All-pure One, cover me also with thy palms, and keep me.[^2577]

*Both now:*
I, wretched one, being frequently submerged by the waves of life to the harm of my mind, and ever tempest-tossed by enemy bondages, now cry unto thee: O Theotokos, help me.[^2578]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3. Podoben: "Of the divine faith" (Бж҃твенныѧ):*[^2579]
Thou wast the divine tabernacle of the Word, O only all-pure Virgin Mother, who in purity didst surpass the Angels. Cleanse me, who above all have become defiled by carnal transgressions, with the divine waters *On Sunday at Compline, Tone 3.*

of thy prayers, granting great mercy, O Pure One.[^2580]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*As of old Thou didst bedew the three pious youths in the Chaldean flame with the bright fire of the Godhead, so also illuminate us who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.*[^2581]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* Assemblies of deceitful demons have surrounded me, and carnal throngs of passions; but as thou didst bear Christ, Who is mighty in battle, deliver me from frequent griefs and fierce assaults.[^2582]

*Troparion 2:* He Who alone is unapproachable unto the cherubic nature and awesome unto the Angelic Ranks, took up His abode in thee, O Pure One, and renewed us; by Him show me, O Lady, to be fearsome unto invisible foes.[^2583]

*Glory:*
Thou wast an intellectual paradise, O Virgin, that sprouted the Tree of life, partaking whereof Adam was delivered from the deadly fruit. Therefore sweeten me also now, and deliver me from tasting the passions.[^2584]

*Both now:*
Deliver me from the mire of bodily passions and demonic temptations, O thou who didst bear the Deliverer unto men, the dwelling-place of the Most High, and consecrated temple of the God of our fathers.[^2585]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Having united themselves unto the unendurable fire, the youths who stood as champions of piety, being unharmed by the flame, sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^2586]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* O cloud of light of Him Who made the great lights in the world, both of the day and of the night: dispel by thy rays every darkening of all the passions of my transgressions, and show me to be a son of light, O Mother of God.[^2587]

*Troparion 2:* By the rain that descended from heaven into thy womb, O Theotokos, enlighten and bedew my soul and my heart; and extinguish the flame of passions and afflictions, that I may warmly glorify thee unto all ages.[^2588]

*Glory:*
Ineffably, O Pure One, hast thou given birth unto the Wisdom of God, Which brought together from non-existence all things, both heavenly and earthly nature; by Whose word grant me wisdom and understanding, that I may manifestly sing of thy divine Childbearing.[^2589]

*Both now:*
That peace may be bedewed upon my soul *On Sunday at Compline, Tone 3.*

and body through thy prayers, O Lady, I cry and pray, and that I may swiftly be delivered from sinful diseases and the encounters with malice; for thou art the healing of all men.[^2590]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Upon Mount Sinai Moses beheld thee in the bush, who didst unburnt conceive the Fire of the Godhead in thy womb; and Daniel beheld thee as the unhewn mountain; and Isaiah cried: A rod that sprouted from the root of David.*[^2591]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:* A divinely woven web for Christ wast thou shown to be, from which the Spirit prepared the garment, the divine-originate flesh; clothe me therefore in the garment of chastity, O Pure One, by thy prayers.[^2592]

*Troparion 2:* O mystical Vine, who didst bud forth for us the divine cluster, from which we are watered with the water of incorruption: pour forth into my soul compunction and the wine of purification, by thy supplications.[^2593]

*Glory:*
O pure and holy bridal chamber, wherein God betrothed the nature of men unto Himself, I pray thee: unite me unto thy Son, and make me His own by divine life, through thy supplications.[^2594]

*Both now:*
Having given birth unto Christ, the Fountain of incorruption, O Virgin, thou hast transformed the pasture of corruption of human nature; put to death therefore for me the burning and tumultuous surging of passions, by thy supplications.[^2595]

*Then: "It is truly meet." Trisagion, and after "Our Father": the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^2596]

*ON MONDAY AT MATINS.*[^2597]
*After the 1st Stichologia, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*[^2598]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Sojourning upon earth, O my soul, repent! For dust in the tomb praises not, nor delivers from transgressions. Cry out unto Christ God: O Knower of hearts, I have sinned against Thee! Before Thou judgest me, spare me, O God, and have mercy on me.[^2599]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^2600]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* How long, O my soul, abidest thou in transgressions? How long dost thou accept the postponing of repentance? Bring to mind the coming judgment; cry out unto Christ God: O Knower of hearts, I have sinned! O sinless Lord, have mercy on me.[^2601]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O Theotokos, our refuge and strength, mighty helper of the world! By thy *On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

prayer save thy servants from every distress, O only Blessed One.[^2602]

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*[^2603]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* At the dread tribunal am I reproached without accusers, and condemned without witnesses; for the books of conscience are unrolled, and hidden deeds are uncovered. Therefore before Thou art to examine in that public spectacle what hath been done by me: O God, cleanse me and save me.[^2604]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^2605]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The depth of my transgressions Thou knowest, O Lord! Stretch forth unto me a hand of help, as unto Peter, and save me.[^2606]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^2607]

*Martyrikon:* The good courage of your endurance hath conquered the crafts of the author of evil, O all-lauded passion-bearers! Therefore have ye been deemed worthy of eternal blessedness. But pray ye unto the Lord to save the flock of Christ-loving people, being witnesses of truth.[^2608]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Rejoice, O Mary, Virgin Mother, holy mountain, paradise of Eden, from whom was born Christ God, the unseeded Word, through Whom life blossomed forth for the world.[^2609]

*After the 3rd Stichologia, Sessional Hymn, Tone 3. Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^2610]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* When we stand before Thine unbribable tribunal and divine judgment, and the heavenly powers shall stand before Thee with trembling: how shall I appear before Thee then, O most righteous Judge, having deeds worthy of condemnation and fire! But by the prayers of Thine Angels, spare me then, and save me.[^2611]

*Another Sessional Hymn, to the Bodiless Powers:*
*Tone the same.*
Heavenly and earthly things hast Thou brought into being by Thy word, as almighty; and the angelic choirs, standing before Thee with trembling, offer unceasing praise unto Thee, O Master, illuminating all the ends of the earth with Thy light. With them we also cry unto Thee: Save us by Thy tender compassion.[^2612]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
As an uncultivated vine, O Virgin, thou hast budded forth the fairest Cluster, which pours forth for us the wine of salvation, making glad the souls and bodies of all. Therefore, ever blessing thee as the cause of good things, with the Angel we cry unto thee: Rejoice, thou who art full of grace.[^2613]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His holy Martyrs.*
*(The work of kyr Joseph.) Tone 3.*[^2614]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Having covered the chariots of Pharaoh with the deep, O Lord, Thou didst divide the sea, and didst save the people of Israel who praised Thee with songs.*[^2615]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^2616]

*Canon 1, Ode 1, Troparion 1:* Deliver me from the darkness of transgressions, O Sinless One, which without understanding I have committed, granting me tears of compunction, as once unto the harlot.[^2617]

*Troparion 2:* Show me, who have become a den of robbers through improper deeds, to be Thy temple through virtues, O Lover of mankind, Who willingly wast born in a cave.[^2618]

*Martyrikon 1:* Guided by the divine Spirit, ye traversed the storm of tortures, O divinely inspired martyrs, and reached the divine haven.[^2619]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having been enlightened by the grace of the Spirit, O martyrs, ye fled from the deepest darkness of the frenzy of idols, rejoicing, O all-lauded ones.[^2620]

*Theotokion:*
O all-holy and all-pure One, helper of sinners, restoration of the falling: grant me compunctionate repentance, who have sinned much.[^2621]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Bodiless Angels.*
*(Whose acrostic is: I make a third song to the Bodiless.)*[^2622]
*(The work of Theophanes.) Tone 3.*[^2623]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Let us sing a new song, O ye peoples, unto Him that was born of the Virgin for our salvation, and made earthly things one with heavenly things; for He hath been glorified.*[^2624]

*Refrain: Holy Archangels and Angels, pray to God for us.*[^2625]

*Canon 2, Ode 1, Troparion 1:* Being the unoriginate Light and the Giver of all manifestation of light, O Christ God: enlighten my thought by the intercession of Thy powers.[^2626]

*Troparion 2:* Rejoicing in a manner befitting angels, through all-rich communion the angelic choirs are mystically illuminated by Thy fair radiances, O Savior.[^2627]

*Troparion 3:* As good and life-creating, the almighty Spirit of the Father hath beneficially established the heavenly powers in the unfailing divine radiances.[^2628]

*Theotokion:*
O Gabriel, leader of the ranks of God, of the immaterial choirs, and now most rich one: cry out a joyous voice unto her that is favored with grace: Rejoice, O All-immaculate One.[^2629]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Establish me, O Lord, Who alone art rich in mercy, and stretch forth unto me a hand as unto Peter, and save me.*[^2630]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 1, Ode 3, Troparion 1:* As Thou didst save Peter when he was sinking, O Lover of mankind, so also lead me up from the depth of my transgressions.[^2631]

*Troparion 2:* The deceitful one hath slain me, killing me with passions; but do Thou, O Giver of life, quicken me by forms of repentance.[^2632]

*Martyrikon 1:* Being unjustly burned with material fire, O martyrs, ye were immaterially inflamed with desire for the Immaterial One.[^2633]

*Martyrikon 2:* Being torn asunder, the martyrs destroyed the tearing asunder and the snare of the deceitful one, and were deemed worthy of crowns.[^2634]

*Theotokion:*
O Mary, called after the Lord, free me who am held in sway by many soul-corrupting passions, by thy prayers.[^2635]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*Thou hast broken the bow of the enemy, and crushed the shields by Thy might, O Master Christ, our confirmation: Holy art Thou, O Lord.*[^2636]

*Canon 2, Ode 3, Troparion 1:* Flowing streams and rivers of goodness hast Thou revealed, O Good One: the angelic choirs, shining with the ray of Thy hidden mystery.[^2637]

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest manifestly show the treasures of Thy wealth and might, O Master Christ, Thou hast brought forth the intellectual powers as partakers of Thy glory.[^2638]

*Troparion 3:* Standing before Thee in trembling as servants, the angelic splendors ever sing of Thy power, O Christ, of immeasurable might.[^2639]

*Theotokion:*
He took whole man from thee immutably, and the same Master of all, O Mother of God, hath revealed thee as a fountain of grace.[^2640]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Wast Thou angry with the rivers, O Lord? Was Thine anger against the rivers, or Thine indignation against the sea?*[^2641]

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Troparion 1:* Loose the bonds of my passions, O Lord, binding me with repentance, and reveal me as a partaker of Thy good things.[^2642]

*Troparion 2:* Cause radiant forms of repentance to dawn upon me, O Christ Who art the Sun, driving away the deep night of mine evils.[^2643]

*Martyrikon 1:* Ye appeared, O wise sufferers, like coals burning up the matter of godlessness, and enlightening those that are in darkness.[^2644]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Martyrikon 2:* Ye were assimilated unto the death of Christ, O martyrs, having mingled yourselves with various pains; therefore have ye inherited divine life.[^2645]

*Theotokion:*
