Grant me true repentance, O Pure One, and still the tumult of my passions, O protectress of those who sin.[^2646]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*I heard the strange and ineffable mystery of Thy dispensation, and was afraid; and rejoicing I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.*[^2647]

*Canon 2, Ode 4, Troparion 1:* Clouds receiving the three-sun illumination, ye were borne along by divine will, O threefold adornments, piloted by the power of the Spirit.[^2648]

*Troparion 2:* Into the world are the Angels sent unto those that are about to believe in Thee, as guardians of the salvation of the pious, keeping Thy servants, O Savior.[^2649]

*Troparion 3:* The Angels partake of the immaterial mystery, being illuminated by the approach of Thy Godhead, O Christ, crying: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^2650]

*Theotokion:*
As a servant supplicant do I now come under thy roof, O All-immaculate One! Deliver me, O Mother of God, from the confusion of passions, who didst bear the Cause of dispassion.[^2651]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*With Thy never-setting light, O Christ God, illuminate my humble soul, and guide it unto Thy fear; for a light are Thy commandments.*[^2652]

*Canon 1, Ode 5, Troparion 1:* Set me not naked of good deeds at Thy tribunal, O Lover of mankind! But clothe me in the garment of divine deeds through repentance.[^2653]

*Troparion 2:* I have been wounded with the weapon of passions, and have fallen into the pit of despair; despise me not, O Master, but heal me with the herb of conversion.[^2654]

*Martyrikon 1:* Both being fiercely scraped with nails, and slain with the sword, O sufferers, and exhausted with wounds, ye bowed not the knee unto idols.[^2655]

*Martyrikon 2:* The world ever gloris in your sufferings, holy martyrs, for ye have found a transcendent dwelling with the Angels.[^2656]

*Theotokion:*
O thou who alone on earth didst give birth in the flesh unto the Word by a word, and didst remain a virgin: [deliver] me from irrational [passions]...[^2657]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...deliver me from [irrational] deeds, that in rational words of honor I may praise thee.[^2658]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*Upon earth, O Invisible One, Thou wast seen, and with men Thou didst willingly dwell, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early at dawn unto Thee, we praise Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^2659]

*Canon 2, Ode 5, Troparion 1:* Being enlightened with the three-sun light of the Father, the Son, and the Spirit, O faithful, let us praise the one Godhead with the angelic hosts.[^2660]

*Troparion 2:* The angelic ranks, gathering like lamps, manifestly enriched by the never-setting radiance of the pre-divine Godhead, proclaim Thee.[^2661]

*Troparion 3:* The Seraphim teach us to honor the one pre-unoriginate Principle, crying aloud manifestly the thrice-holy hymn, Angels enlightened by the Spirit.[^2662]

*Theotokion:*
Having descended from heaven, O all-pure Theotokos, the Archangel Gabriel announced unto thee the good tidings of giving birth without seed unto the Giver of life, O Maiden.[^2663]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*O Lover of mankind, despise not him who hath come unto the end of the ages and is in peril of perishing through the surges of afflictions, crying out: Save me, O Savior, as Thou didst save the Prophet from the sea-monster.*[^2664]

*Canon 1, Ode 6, Troparion 1:* I was exalted in mind like the ancient Pharisee; through transgression I fell down fiercely, and seeing me the deceiver rejoices. O Christ, Who didst humble him by the Cross: have compassion on me who now have humbled myself.[^2665]

*Troparion 2:* Having been a lover of sin in life as none other, O Master, I, wretched one, have exhausted Thy long-suffering, and yet I am unfeeling; but convert me by Thy tender compassion.[^2666]

*Martyrikon 1:* With wrathful purpose the transgressors of the law condemned the divinely wise, and by fire, burning, and piercings they showed them to be truly brighter than gold and co-heirs with Christ.[^2667]

*Martyrikon 2:* Having passed through a tribunal full of struggles and exceedingly great ascetic feats, and wounds surpassing nature, ye conquered the prince of darkness, O sufferers, and received crowns from God.[^2668]

*Theotokion:*
The ark of the covenant, the truly holy table, our mercy-seat, the ensouled church of God, the all-golden lampstand, the true Mother of the Creator do we praise in song.[^2669]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*Jonah, being a natural image of the dwelling in the lowest Hades, cried out: Lead up my life from corruption, O Lover of mankind.*[^2670]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 2, Ode 6, Troparion 1:* The prime and all-effective radiance of the Godhead the Archangels and Angels powerfully sing, calling upon the Lord with unceasing voices together with Principalities, and Authorities, and Powers. *(Twice)*[^2671]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who didst invisibly adorn the noetic world in good order with all-diverse rank, vouchsafe that the honorable Church may be conformed unto this good order.[^2672]

*Theotokion:*
The Word that took up His abode in thee showed thee to be an ever-flowing fountain of healings; heal therefore the wounds of my soul, O Virgin Theotokos.[^2673]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The three youths in the furnace, prefiguring the Trinity, trampled the fiery threatening, and singing cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.*[^2674]

*Canon 1, Ode 7, Troparion 1:* My time is ending, O my soul! The departure is at the doors; show forth therefore the fruits of repentance. Before the doors are shut, cry out unto Christ: O Lord, save me.[^2675]

*Troparion 2:* Let us renew our hearts with the plow of true knowledge, sowing the wheat of repentance, that we may reap the ear of righteousness, O tillers of our souls in Christ.[^2676]

*Martyrikon 1:* Luminaries of piety, adversaries of impiety, enrichers of the poor, O martyrs of the Lord! Enrich with virtues my most wretched soul that is destitute.[^2677]

*Martyrikon 2:* O Thou Who didst save the publican that sighed from the depths, receive also my small sighing, and save me for the sake of the passion-bearers who glorified Thee with their members, O Christ.[^2678]

*Theotokion:*
O fountain pouring forth the water of remission, dry up the torrents of my transgressions, granting me a cloud of tears, that I may ever praise thee as the Theotokos.[^2679]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*The proud tormentor became a plaything of children; for having trampled the sevenfold flame as dust, they sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.*[^2680]

*Canon 2, Ode 7, Troparion 1:* Beholding with steadfast mind and valiant eye, through communion in the primal splendor ye become secondary lights, O angelic choirs, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers. *(Twice)*[^2681]

*Troparion 2:* Guided by unwavering desire and unflagging love, *On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...and by the beauties of the Creator, ye became secondary luminaries by position, O angelic choirs, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^2682]

*Theotokion:*
Incarnate didst thou bear the unoriginate Word, O Theotokos, Who is from the Father without mother before all ages, by a divine birth. Blessed, O Lady, is the Fruit of thy womb.[^2683]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto the Lord Who by divine power descended into the flame unto the Hebrew youths, and appeared: O ye priests, bless Him, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^2684]

*Canon 1, Ode 8, Troparion 1:* Through mocking, Ham became a servant by his father's sentence; what wilt thou do, O my soul, having enslaved thyself to passions, and laughing disorderly, and having no shame before the Heavenly Father?[^2685]

*Troparion 2:* Having conceived enmity foolishly, Cain became a fratricide; unto him hast thou been shown like, O my soul, not having slain another, but thyself, through pleasure-loving and the deceits of life.[^2686]

*Martyrikon 1:* The chosen pearls of the Lord, honorable vessels shining with the radiance of lamps of divine grace, the passion-bearers of Christ, having assembled in faith, let us honor worthily.[^2687]

*Martyrikon 2:* O Word Who didst receive the blood of the wise passion-bearers more than incense, by their supplications save those who fall down before Thee in repentance, as the only Merciful One.[^2688]

*Theotokion:*
By the excess of a prodigal life, I have now drawn near unto Hades; having exceeding great tender compassion, O Maiden, as having ineffably given birth unto the compassionate God, have compassion and save me.[^2689]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*With immaterial flame the God-beholding youths withered the flame of material fire, and sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^2690]

*Canon 2, Ode 8, Troparion 1:* As Creator of all, Thou didst conceive the Angels in mind, and they stood before Thee with fear, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^2691]

*Troparion 2:* By communion with the divine fire, having become like a flame, the heavenly minds cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^2692]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst constitute the immaterial and noetic Angels before *On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...all visible things, who unceasingly cry unto Thee: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^2693]

*Canon 2, Ode 8, Troparion 4:* Thou didst constitute the immaterial and noetic Angels before all visible things, who unceasingly cry unto Thee: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; praise and exalt Him above all unto the ages.[^2694]

*Theotokion:*
The Word of the Father, incarnate beyond word, hast thou born, O Virgin Mother of God, Whom all works praise as Lord and exalt above all unto all ages.[^2695]

*Then: "My soul doth magnify the Lord."*
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Upon Mount Sinai Moses beheld thee in the bush, who didst unburnt conceive the Fire of the Godhead in thy womb; and Daniel beheld thee as the unhewn mountain; and Isaiah cried: A rod that sprouted from the root of David.*[^2696]

*Canon 1, Ode 9, Troparion 1:* Jacob received the birthright of old through temperance; but Esau gave away his primogeniture, having not restrained his belly. How evil is intemperance, and how great is temperance! Flee from what is unfitting; love, O my soul, the increase of good things.[^2697]

*Troparion 2:* As having endured many things, blameless Job was crowned; for the torrents of temptations that flowed together did not shake the pillar of his heart. Emulate him, O my soul, ever remaining unchangeable against the crafts of the deceitful one.[^2698]

*Martyrikon 1:* Sharpened by the fire of the divine Spirit, ye appeared manifestly as swords, cutting down the opposing legions, O passion-bearers of Christ; and being glorified by great victories, ye were crowned by the all-powerful hand of the King of all.[^2699]

*Martyrikon 2:* O blameless multitude of martyrs! Pray the Master and God, Who received your ineffable labors and united you with the hosts of the bodiless, to cleanse the multitude of my measureless evils.[^2700]

*Theotokion:*
O radiant chamber of the Master, show me to be a house of light; O impassable gate, open unto me the paths of repentance; O holy earth, guide me unto the land of the meek, O Lady, and show me free from every dominion of passions.[^2701]

*Other Heirmos [for Canon 2]:*
*Wounded, O Pure One, by the sweet dart of thy most pure childbirth, marveling at thy much-desired beauty, with angelic songs we worthily magnify thee as Mother of God.*[^2702]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 2, Ode 9, Troparion 1:* O choirs that now stand before the great and prime Light, become most mighty intercessors for all of us who magnify you as much as is possible, O most divine Angels of God. *(Twice)*[^2703]

*Troparion 2:* Having been deemed worthy to clearly glorify the equal-in-power Trinity, being primarily illumined by Its radiance: make us, who piously magnify you, worthy to be illumined with secondary radiance.[^2704]

*Theotokion:*
We all know thee to be the foundation of our salvation; for thy most pure childbearing, by divine-working blood, hath saved those who sing of thee, O Theotokos, and faithfully glorify thee.[^2705]

*Then: "It is truly meet." Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^2706]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 3:*[^2707]

*Sticheron 1:* Gather my scattered mind, O Lord, and cleanse my frozen heart, giving me repentance as unto Peter, sighing as unto the publican, and tears as unto the harlot, that with a great voice I may cry unto Thee: O God, save me, as the only compassionate One and Lover of mankind.[^2708]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^2709]

*Sticheron 2:* Many a time while performing the singing, I was found fulfilling sin; uttering the song with my tongue, but thinking unfitting things in my soul. But amend both, O Christ God, through repentance, and save me.[^2710]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^2711]

*Martyrikon:* The memory of the holy passion-bearers, come, all ye people, let us honor; for having become a spectacle unto Angels and men, they received crowns of victory from Christ; and they pray for our souls.[^2712]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O holy, prime-pure boast of the heavenly ranks, song of the Apostles and fulfillment of the Prophets, O Lady, receive our prayers.[^2713]

*Then: "It is good to give praise." Trisagion; after "Our Father": troparion, and Litany. After this, the First Hour.*[^2714]

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 3:*[^2715]
*Troparion 1:* Adam our forefather, who rejected Thy commandment, O Christ, *On Monday at the Liturgy, Tone 3.*

...Thou didst cast out of paradise; but the thief, O Compassionate One, who confessed Thee on the Cross, Thou didst settle therein, crying: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy kingdom.[^2716]

*Troparion 2:* Having overlooked the multitude of mine evils according to the multitude of Thy mercy, O Christ, save me; and in the day of judgment deliver me, O Savior, from condemnation and eternal torment, that I may hymn Thy goodness.[^2717]

*Troparion 3 [to the Bodiless Powers]:* Cherubim and Seraphim, Thrones, Archangels, Powers, and Principalities, and Dominions, Angels and all Authorities: pray the Creator to overlook then my transgressions, when He shall come to judge the earth, as the Lover of mankind.[^2718]

*Martyrikon:* Having dipped a purple robe in their sacred blood, the passion-bearers of Christ were truly deemed worthy to reign with the King of hosts in a manner befitting God; and they ask remission of transgressions for us who ever worthily honor them.[^2719]

*Glory:* Piously singing of the one tri-hypostatic Godhead, O faithful: the Father, and the Word, and the Holy Comforter Spirit, let us cry out with unceasing voices in the manner of angels: Holy, Holy, Holy art Thou, O God; save our souls.[^2720]

*Both now:* O thou who didst bear the Light of Light, radiant cloud! Having guided me, who am darkened by passions and the pleasures of life, unto the light, direct me, O Pure One, and pray that I may attain the glory which those who lived well have now obtained.[^2721]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^2722]

*On Monday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 3.*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "In the likeness of the Cross" (Крⷭ҇тообра́знѡ):*[^2723]

*Sticheron 1:* Beholding in me slothfulness toward profitable works, and an inclination toward evil and worse things, the manifold serpent transforms himself, displaying the sweetness of sin, but his own bitter working, contrary to the divine commandments; therefore *On Monday Evening, Tone 3.*

...he urges me on by deceitful custom to accept evils as good things, O Christ.[^2724]

*Sticheron 2:* Having eagerly walked every path of lawlessness and sin, and having abandoned the upright path unto the end, I have now drawn near unto the gates of death; being straitened I cry: O Way of our life, my all-good Jesus! Convert me unto the breadth of true repentance and save me; grant me amendment, and vouchsafe me divine forgiveness before death.[^2725]

*Sticheron 3:* Having been slain by various sins, and by multitudes of transgressions, and by great impropriety, I lie dead, helpless indeed; but my one hope lives, which is toward Thy tender compassion, O Christ, Who givest breath and life together unto the dead, and puttest to death the passions that have slain us. Therefore anticipate me, and snatch me from eternal death.[^2726]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Forerunner John:*[^2727]
*Tone the same.*

*Sticheron 1 to the Forerunner:* O Forerunner, Who didst loose thy mother's barrenness and thy father's speechlessness at thy nativity: loose the childlessness of my heart and the irrationality of my soul by thy acceptable prayers unto the compassionate Son of the Father, the Master of all and God, Whom the Mind begot dispassionately as the Word; and strengthen me both to speak and to do the things pleasing unto Him.[^2728]

*Sticheron 2 to the Forerunner:* O thou who didst prepare the paths for Christ and make straight His ways, before Whose face thou wast sent: show my heart also to be a good pathway for Him by thy prayers and helpful manifestations; O thou who wast shown worthy of His honorable feet, make me worthy to walk upon the heavenly earth, where the feet of the meek tread, that with love I may honor thee as an intercessor.[^2729]

*Sticheron 3 to the Forerunner:* Thou didst emulate the life of Angels upon earth, O completion of the Prophets, and first martyr of the New Testament, who wast the first preacher unto those under the earth of the descent of the divine Word thither, attested by Christ, friend of the Lamb and Deliverer! From all snares of the enemy, and from his entwined temptations, deliver thy servant, O John the Baptist, by thy prayers.[^2730]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O cloud of the never-setting Light, Maiden favored with divine joy, blessed Mary! Cause the light of repentance to shine upon me who am foolishly held fast in the darkness of sin; and by thy supplications deliver me from the fire of Gehenna and the lightless darkness, and show me, who flee beneath thy protection, O All-pure One, to be a partaker of the never-ending day, O Virgin.[^2731]

*Then: "O Gladsome Radiance." The Prokeimenon of the day.*[^2732]

*On Monday Evening, Tone 3.*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 3:*[^2733]

*Sticheron 1:* An evening song do we offer unto Thee, O Christ, with incense and spiritual songs: have mercy and save our souls.[^2734]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, who dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^2735]

*Sticheron 2:* Save me, O Lord my God, for Thou art salvation unto all! The tempest of passions troubles me, and the burden of mine iniquities drowns me; stretch forth unto me a hand of help, and lead me up unto the light of repentance, as the only compassionate One and Lover of mankind.[^2736]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^2737]

*Martyrikon:* Great is the power of Thy martyrs, O Christ! For lying in the tombs, they drive away spirits, and they abolished the enemy's power, having contended for piety by faith in the Trinity.[^2738]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O holy Theotokos among women, unwedded Mother! Pray Him Whom thou didst bear, the King and God, that He may save us, as the Lover of mankind.[^2739]

*Then: "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart," and the rest.*[^2740]

*On Monday at Compline,*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^2741]
*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by His divine bidding gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, most glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He hath been glorified.*[^2742]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^2743]

*Troparion 1:* Having spent my life in slothfulness, I have drawn near unto the end of my life; but do thou thyself, O Pure One, at least in the last time, grant me compunction, I pray, that I may weep bitterly over my measureless transgressions.[^2744]

*Troparion 2:* Having foolishly defiled the beauty of the image by the passions of my flesh, O Virgin, I was terrified of the indignation of God, and of the dread fiery threatening; but do thou thyself have mercy on me who flee unto thee.[^2745]

*Glory:* Having bowed my knees, wretched one, I ask thy help, O all-holy Virgin! Hear thou my suffering soul, and dispel the pressing cloud of my sorrow by the radiance of thy prayers.[^2746]

*Both now:* Grant that the furrows of my passionate *On Monday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...soul may be richly watered with tears, and vouchsafe me, O Lady, to bring forth fruit a hundredfold, and fill my heart with all gladness, that I may glorify thee.[^2747]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Master, Who didst bring all things into being from non-existence, created by the Word, perfected by the Spirit: establish me in Thy love.*[^2748]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Grant me a cloud of tears, O good Theotokos, and with them quench the furnace of my passions, and wash away every defilement of my soul.[^2749]

*Troparion 2:* In lawlessness have I defiled the nobility of my soul, O Pure One, and I tremble at the inquiry, when the Word shall examine my worthiness of the image.[^2750]

*Glory:* The storm of transgressions having encompassed me now, hath brought me down into the depth of despair; but stretch forth unto me a hand, O Pure One, and guide me unto repentance.[^2751]

*Both now:* Deliver thy servant, O all-immaculate One, from Gehenna and from every other threatening at the hour of judgment, and make me a partaker of the kingdom of thy Son and God.[^2752]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast set toward us a steadfast love, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine only-begotten Son unto death for our sake. Therefore we cry unto Thee in thanksgiving: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^2753]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The waters of unfitting deeds, O Pure One, have come in unto my wretched soul; therefore held fast by thoughts of mire, I cry out with pain: Despise me not, O Lady, thy servant.[^2754]

*Troparion 2:* Noetic beasts have now unmercifully compassed me, striving unsparingly to snatch away my humble soul, O all-immaculate One; but crush their soul-captivating jaws, O Pure One.[^2755]

*Glory:* Be merciful unto thy servant, O Lady, I pray, and snatch thy people from the expected threatening, O All-pure One, that we may cry unto thee in thanksgiving: Glory to thee, the Queen of all.[^2756]

*Both now:* When in the night the Bridegroom shall come to judge the earth, then vouchsafe me, O Pure One, to go forth unto His meeting with a radiant lamp, and to bow down before His coming.[^2757]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Upon earth, O Invisible One, Thou wast seen, and with men Thou didst willingly dwell, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early at dawn unto Thee, we praise Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^2758]

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Many dogs have truly encompassed me; a congregation of evil spirits hath surrounded me; but dispel their counsels now, O Pure One.[^2759]

*Troparion 2:* Having dug a pit for me now, the evil one attempts to cast me therein; but by thy right hand, O Lady, let him fall into the ditch which he hath made.[^2760]

*Glory:* May I not be reproved by the wrath of thy Son at the time of His coming, nor chastened by His anger, O All-praised One! But save me by thy supplications.[^2761]

*Both now:* Behold, O Pure One, the infirmity, behold the humiliation of my wretched soul, and the rising up of bodiless enemies, and deliver me from their harm.[^2762]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The uttermost abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch forth Thy high arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.*[^2763]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* By the bidding of the Creator of all, when my soul shall be parted from the flesh, O all-immaculate One: free me from the hand of those who hate me, O all-praised Theotokos.[^2764]

*Troparion 2:* Dry up the turbid rivers of mine evil deeds, O Mother of God, with streams of compunction, and direct me unto the water of relief in the day of judgment.[^2765]

*Glory:* Thou knowest my psychic infirmity, and the weakness of my mind, O Pure One, and the frailty of my flesh; therefore save thy servant, for I have acquired thee as an unconquerable protectress.[^2766]

*Both now:* Grant me streams of spiritual tears, O Maiden, wherewith I may wash away the mire of my transgressions, and the tumult of passions, O all-immaculate One, and bodily defilements.[^2767]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3. Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^2768]
O thou who didst bear the Merciful One in thy womb! Have compassion upon him who flees beneath thy protection, and asks from his soul for thy divine help, O Lady, and make him worthy of mercy; and when we stand before the Master of creation, deliver me, O Pure One, from eternal fire and every condemnation.[^2769]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Of old, the three youths bowed not down to the golden image, the Persian object of worship, singing in the midst of the furnace: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.*[^2770]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The waters of transgressions, O Pure One, have poured forth unto my soul...[^2771]

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...and the uttermost abyss hath compassed me; but deliver me from this tempest.[^2772]

*Troparion 2:* Sprinkle me, O Pure One, with the blood from the ribs of thy Offspring, according to the multitude of thy mercy; and wash me with streams of tears, and cleanse me from every defilement.[^2773]

*Glory:* Grant contrition to my soul, and humility to my heart, O All-pure One, that I may be delivered from every craftiness of those who ever mercilessly hunt me.[^2774]

*Both now:* As the merciful Mother of God, vouchsafe mercy unto thy servants, O Lady, who with faith cry aloud unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^2775]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*United with unendurable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remained unharmed by the flame and sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all for ever.*[^2776]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Break the arrows of the archer, O Pure One, by the dominion of thy Son, and let his unrighteousness now descend upon his head, that I may sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2777]

*Troparion 2:* Illumine my darkened heart with thy light, O Maiden, and with the armor of light open unto me the doors of light as I cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2778]

*Glory:* Having fallen asleep in death, I lie in the tomb of despair; but do thou thyself raise me up, O Virgin, and make me worthy to sing vigilantly: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2779]

*Both now:* Cease not, O Pure One, to pray for those who honor thee in compunction, that they may be delivered from the snares of the devil, crying unto thy Son: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2780]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and the letter of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: Every male that opens the womb is holy to God. Therefore the firstborn Word of the unoriginate Father, the Son Who is the firstborn of an unwedded Mother, do we magnify.*[^2781]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The surging of passions and the confusion of defiled thoughts overwhelm my soul, and the tempest of evil men ever troubles me; but as a lover of mankind, O Virgin, quickly deliver me from the distress that holds me fast.[^2782]

*Troparion 2:* O humble soul, depart from thine evil deeds, and cease...

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...cease from malice and from angering God; and diligently receive His commandments, for thou hast the Theotokos directing thy paths.[^2783]

*Glory:* As she that bare the Lord of all, free me from passions and grievous sins, and enrich my whole self with good works for thy great mercy's sake, that rejoicing I may magnify thee with love, O All-immaculate One.[^2784]

*Both now:* The end draws nigh, O my soul, the judgment seat is at the doors; depart from shameful deeds, and take hold of a good life; for thou hast the Theotokos as a helper, delivering thee from every affliction.[^2785]

*Then: It is truly meet... Trisagion. After the Our Father: the troparion and the rest as usual, and the dismissal.*[^2786]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*[^2787]
*After the 1st Stichology, Sessional Hymns of Compunction, Tone 3:*
Abiding upon the earth, O my soul, repent; for the dust in the grave sings not, nor delivers from transgressions; cry aloud unto Christ God: O Knower of hearts, I have sinned against Thee; before Thou judgest me, spare me, O God, and have mercy on me.[^2788]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^2789]
How long, O my soul, dost thou remain in transgressions? How long dost thou accept the postponement of repentance? Bring to mind the coming judgment, and cry aloud unto Christ God: O Knower of hearts, I have sinned; O Sinless Lord, have mercy on me.[^2790]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Marveling at the beauty of thy virginity and the exceedingly bright splendor of thy purity, Gabriel cried aloud unto thee, O Theotokos: What worthy praise shall I bring unto thee? Or what shall I name thee? I am in perplexity and I am amazed. Therefore, as I was commanded, I cry aloud unto thee: Rejoice, thou who art full of grace![^2791]

*After the 2nd Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*
At the dread judgment seat I am convicted without accusers, and without witnesses am I condemned; for the books of conscience are opened, and secret deeds are disclosed. Before, therefore, Thou shalt examine upon that public spectacle the deeds done by me, O God, cleanse me and save me.[^2792]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^2793]
Thou knowest the depth of my transgressions, O Lord; give me a hand of help, as unto Peter, and save me.[^2794]

*Verse: Wondrous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^2795]
*Martyrikon:* Ye shine through faith as exceedingly radiant luminaries, O holy ones, physicians of the infirm, all-praised passion-bearers! For ye feared not the torments of the torturers, and cast down the impiety of idols, possessing an invincible victory: the true Cross.[^2796]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Receive him that flees with faith beneath thy protection, O Lady, and despise me not, nor overlook me, O Good One, who pray in repentance. Receive the prayer from my unworthy lips, and by thine intercession deliver me from snares, that with boldness I may cry unto thee: Rejoice, thou who art full of grace![^2797]

*After the 3rd Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3.*[^2798]
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^2799]
Having fled beneath thy protection, with faith we call from the depth of our soul: O divinely-blessed Prophet and Forerunner! Still the confusion of assaults and tribulations, and the tempest of diseases; and make ineffectual the crafty counsels of the enemies, asking for us great mercy.[^2800]

Shake off thy despondency through repentance, O wretched soul, before thy departure; turn back with weeping, crying unto the guileless Jesus, the Lover of mankind: I have sinned against Thee, O Master! But as Thou art compassionate, save me through the prayers of the holy Forerunner, Who alone art sinless.[^2801]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Incomprehensible and unsearchable, O Lady full of divine grace, is the dread mystery of God accomplished concerning thee; for having conceived the Uncontainable One, thou gavest birth unto Him, clothed in flesh from thy most pure blood; Whom do thou ever entreat, O Pure One, together with the Forerunner, that our souls may be saved.[^2802]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His Holy Martyrs.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Attend unto my groanings, O Word of God.)*[^2803]
*(The work of Joseph.) Tone 3.*[^2804]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Marvelous in glory, doing wonders art Thou, O God, Who didst turn the deep into dry land, and didst drown the chariots, and savedst the people that sing unto Thee as our King and God.*[^2805]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Troparion 1:* Let us ourselves weep from our whole soul before the end, O faithful! The Bridegroom draws nigh; let us kindle our deeds as radiant lamps, that together we may attain the divine bridal chamber...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...that together we may attain the divine bridal chamber.[^2806]

*Troparion 2:* Having repented of old from his whole soul, Manasseh was saved; for, bound fast in the midst of fetters, he cried aloud unto the only Master. Emulate him, O soul, and thou shalt easily be saved.[^2807]

*Martyrikon 1:* Blindness of eyes, severance of hands, cutting out of tongue, and amputation of feet, breaking of thighs and arms did the divine sufferers endure, giving thanks unto Jesus Christ.[^2808]

*Martyrikon 2:* A place of healing hath the shrine of your relics appeared unto us all, the faithful, O holy martyrs; from whence we draw healings of souls and bodies, who ever worthily honor you.[^2809]

*Theotokion:* Carrying Christ, the Manna of immortality, O spiritual urn: deliver me from the bitterness of soul-destroying passions, O All-hymned Virgin, that with faith I may piously give glory to thee.[^2810]

*Another Canon, to the Forerunner John.*[^2811]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by divine beck gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel, this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; unto Him alone do we sing, for He hath been glorified.*[^2812]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*[^2813]
*Canon 2, Ode 1, Troparion 1:* Divine offspring of a barren womb: pray to God that I may bring forth fruits of virtues, loosing the barrenness of my sin, and driving away the gloom from my mind, O Forerunner of the Lord.[^2814]

*Canon 2, Ode 1, Troparion 2:* As a great forerunner star of the Sun of glory, Who enlightens all the earth, hast thou appeared on earth through faith. Do thou therefore pray to Him, O Forerunner, to enlighten my soul darkened by evil thoughts.[^2815]

*Canon 2, Ode 1, Troparion 3:* O Prophet who by the divine Spirit didst proclaim life drawing nigh unto those in Hades: quicken my slain soul by thy supplications, and raise me up, I pray, as from the tomb of transgressions, O glorious Forerunner.[^2816]

*Theotokion:* Together with Archangels and Angels, O Virgin, and with all the Saints, entreat the Lord Who through thee appeared unto us, we pray, that those who confess thee as true Theotokos...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...may be delivered from tribulations.[^2817]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O barren and childless soul, acquire glorious fruit, and crying out in bearing good children: I am established through Thee, O God! There is none holy, there is none righteous above Thee, O Lord.*[^2818]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 3, Troparion 1:* Senselessly have I rejected the Law of God, and I am to be condemned, and what I shall do, I know not. O most righteous Judge, have compassion on me and save me in Thy loving-kindness.[^2819]

*Canon 1, Ode 3, Troparion 2:* Being the Orient of orients, O Lover of mankind: shine forth upon me, I pray, the light of righteousness, delivering me from the darkening of passions and the darkness of torment, O Plenteous in mercy.[^2820]

*Canon 1, Ode 3, Martyrikon 1:* Dwellers of the noetic land, fruitful trees of paradise, holy martyrs, fountains possessing divine water, cups pouring forth holy drink have ye appeared.[^2821]

*Canon 1, Ode 3, Martyrikon 2:* Bearing a single character in many bodies, O passion-bearing martyrs, ye vanquished the ruler of the world of darkness, proclaiming the indivisible Trinity.[^2822]

*Canon 1, Ode 3, Theotokion:* O Virgin Mother most pure, unto us who repent and flee unto Christ, and desire to receive remission of transgressions, make Him merciful unto all, O Lady of all.[^2823]

*Another Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Master, Who didst bring all things out of nothingness, which are created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit: establish me in Thy love.*[^2824]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 3, Troparion 1:* Having been the voice of the Word, O Baptist: direct now unto Him the voices of those who honor thee, and grant us remission of transgressions by thine intercession.[^2825]

*Canon 2, Ode 3, Troparion 2:* I have sinned against Thee and committed iniquity, and grievously, O Savior, have I transgressed and defiled my soul. Therefore do I pray Thee: for Thy Baptist's sake, have compassion upon me.[^2826]

*Canon 2, Ode 3, Troparion 3:* O thou who wast nurtured in the wilderness and art the instructor of the new people: I pray thee, O Forerunner, guide me who have wandered in the wilderness through pleasures into the way of repentance.[^2827]

*Canon 2, Ode 3, Theotokion:* Together with the Apostles and the divine Prophets, with the Martyrs, O All-pure One, and with the heavenly Powers: pray to thy Son to have compassion on us who sing of thee.[^2828]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*The shadowed mountain did Habakkuk foresee, even thy most pure womb, O Pure One...*

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...therefore did he cry: God shall come from Theman, and the Holy One from a mountain shadowed with dense woods.[^2829]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 4, Troparion 1:* O Christ Who of old didst bring forth water from the rock for an intractable and contradictory people: from my dry soul, O God, bring forth a drop of compunction to wash me clean.[^2830]

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Troparion 2:* O Physician of the sick: heal the passions of my heart by Thy compassionate nod, applying thereto, O Savior, as Good One, the plaster of repentance, of divine healing, that in faith I may glorify Thee.[^2831]

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Martyrikon 1:* Having compassionately unyoked the thought of friendship toward the body, O passion-bearers, ye surrendered yourselves to those who wished to torment you. Therefore did ye become close friends unto the Creator.[^2832]

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Martyrikon 2:* Grievous wounds from manifold tortures did ye endure, O sufferers of Christ, and ye received grace from spiritual gifts, driving away the long-enduring sicknesses of our passions.[^2833]

*Canon 1, Ode 4, Theotokion:* Rejoice, O Maiden of many names, from whom God the Word was born, loosing us from irrational and unfitting deeds! Rejoice, radiant cloud that drivest away the clouds of our despondency![^2834]

*Another Heirmos:*
*Thou hast set a firm love toward us, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore unto Thee do we cry, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^2835]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 4, Troparion 1:* Thee, who art the voice of the Word manifested in the flesh, do I entreat, O Forerunner of the Lord: deliver me from irrational deeds, who rationally honor thee and dutifully bless thee in faith.[^2836]

*Canon 2, Ode 4, Troparion 2:* Sigh, O soul, and cry aloud unto God thy Creator: I have sinned, cleanse me, O Christ, and deliver me from dread torment, and from perils and afflictions, through the supplications of the divine Forerunner.[^2837]

*Canon 2, Ode 4, Troparion 3:* Plunged by many waves of grievous passions, and cruelly held fast by tempest, and ever drowning: snatch me away, O Baptist, and guide me unto the haven of repentance.[^2838]

*Canon 2, Ode 4, Theotokion:* Mother of God, chariot higher than the Cherubim: together with the immaterial ministers, and with all the Saints...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...entreat Christ, Whom thou didst bear, O All-immaculate One, to save me who am in despair.[^2839]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Rising early at dawn, we hymn Thee, O Word, only-begotten Son of God! Grant us Thy peace, and have mercy upon us who praise Thee and faithfully worship Thee.*[^2840]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 5, Troparion 1:* Having sprinkled me with the hyssop of repentance, cleanse me from the defilements of passions, that I may appear pure before Thee, when Thou shalt judge all things, O Jesus, by Thy righteous judgment.[^2841]

*Canon 1, Ode 5, Troparion 2:* The wounds of my most wretched soul are become corrupt, O Savior; O Physician of the sick, and Giver of good things, heal and save me, for Thy great mercy's sake.[^2842]

*Canon 1, Ode 5, Martyrikon 1:* The earthen vessels of the passion-bearers were broken upon the earth, but the strength of their soul was strengthened yet more, and shone forth by the power of Christ.[^2843]

*Canon 1, Ode 5, Martyrikon 2:* The shed blood of the Saints sanctified the whole earth, and gave drink to the souls of the faithful, and manifestly dried up the turbid streams of deception.[^2844]

*Canon 1, Ode 5, Theotokion:* By thy birthgiving, O Maiden, thou didst make barren the ancestral curse, and didst pour forth unto us rivers of blessing, who bless thee and glorify thee in faith.[^2845]

*Another Heirmos:*
*Invisible upon earth hast Thou appeared, and willingly hast Thou dwelt with men, O Incomprehensible One! And rising early at dawn unto Thee, we sing of Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^2846]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 5, Troparion 1:* As an Angel upon earth, O blessed one, didst thou live in the flesh; therefore do I pray thee: free my soul from carnal mindedness.[^2847]

*Canon 2, Ode 5, Troparion 2:* Having fallen into the depth of sin, and defiled my soul with pleasures, and being in peril: O Forerunner of the Lord, save me who flee unto thee.[^2848]

*Canon 2, Ode 5, Troparion 3:* Higher than the Prophets hast thou appeared, for thou thyself, O Prophet, didst behold the One Foretold; Whom do thou unceasingly entreat to enlighten our souls.[^2849]

*Canon 2, Ode 5, Theotokion:* Revealed as more spacious than the heavens through the indwelling of the Word within thee, O thou full of divine grace: free me from the sins that oppress me.[^2850]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The deep of passions hath risen up against me, and the tempest of contrary winds; but make haste and save me, O Savior, and deliver me from corruption, as Thou didst save the Prophet from the sea monster.*[^2851]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 6, Troparion 1:* I am darkened by the gloom of sin, and lie entirely inactive; O Christ God, Who wast once wounded with a spear for my sake, have compassion upon me in Thy mercy.[^2852]

*Canon 1, Ode 6, Troparion 2:* I groan and abide in evils; I shed tears, yet tremble not at the judgment, ailing with insensibility. O Word of God, have compassion upon me, and save me by Thy good judgments.[^2853]

*Canon 1, Ode 6, Martyrikon 1:* As lambs crying not out at all, emitting neither word nor sound, O all-glorious sufferers, ye were led to slaughter and to wounds, singing unto Christ.[^2854]

*Canon 1, Ode 6, Martyrikon 2:* Ye were given over unto wild beasts for food, and partook of the depths of the sea with a joyful soul. Therefore did Christ adorn you with incorruptible crowns, O sufferers.[^2855]

*Canon 1, Ode 6, Theotokion:* Door of those who are faithfully saved, Gate through which He alone passed Who was incarnate for our sake: open the gates of righteousness unto us who sing of thee in faith.[^2856]

*Another Heirmos:*
*The uttermost abyss of sins hath compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch forth Thy high arm, O Master, and save me, O Helmsman, as Thou didst Peter.*[^2857]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 6, Troparion 1:* With the waters, thou didst baptize Him that bowed beneath thy hand, the torrent of pleasure; entreat Him, O wise one, to send down water of compunction upon me who have sinned much.[^2858]

*Canon 2, Ode 6, Troparion 2:* In the river, O Forerunner, thou didst wash the abyss of mercy, Jesus the Lover of mankind, Who covers the highest heights of heaven with waters; entreat Him to pour forth forgiveness unto me.[^2859]

*Canon 2, Ode 6, Troparion 3:* The kingdom of heaven is at hand: repent! didst thou cry, O Forerunner! Make those who love and honor thee, and flee beneath thy precious protection, worthy to obtain it.[^2860]

*Canon 2, Ode 6, Theotokion:* O All-pure One, who gavest thy flesh in loan unto the Creator: together with the heavenly Powers, and with all the Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs, entreat Him to have compassion and save me.[^2861]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who didst bedew the furnace flame, and savedst the youths unconsumed: Blessed art Thou for ever, O Lord, the God of our fathers.*[^2862]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 7, Troparion 1:* I am stripped of the garments of incorruption, and have clothed myself in deeds of dishonor; therefore do I cry unto Thee: O compassionate God, illumine me with the garments of virtues.[^2863]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 1, Ode 7, Troparion 2:* With prodigal eyes have I wallowed in the mire, and by the touch of intemperance have I been defiled, and I am become abominable before Thee, O Jesus; receive me as the prodigal.[^2864]

*Canon 1, Ode 7, Martyrikon 1:* Having loved the life on high, ye endured many afflictions, O armorbearers of Christ, divine luminaries! Therefore are ye faithfully blessed.[^2865]

*Canon 1, Ode 7, Martyrikon 2:* Having been enlightened by the radiance of martyrdom, ye shine forth brighter than the sun, and have driven away all darkness of godlessness, O holy martyrs.[^2866]

*Canon 1, Ode 7, Theotokion:* As thou didst give birth unto the Upholder of all: deliver me from the darkness of ignorance and sin which holds me fast, O pure Theotokos, ever-Virgin.[^2867]

*Another Heirmos:*
*The three youths in the furnace, prefiguring the Trinity, trod underfoot the fiery threatening, and singing they cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers.*[^2868]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 7, Troparion 1:* Luminary of the Sun: illumine my soul which is darkened and blinded by slothfulness, directing me unto the path of repentance, O Baptist and Forerunner of Christ.[^2869]

*Canon 2, Ode 7, Troparion 2:* Contemplating the hour of judgment, I am entirely terrified, lying in a multitude of unfitting deeds; O Baptist of the Lord, stand by and deliver me from the fire that lies before me.[^2870]

*Canon 2, Ode 7, Troparion 3:* Protector of my life, my defender, O Forerunner: preserve and shelter me from enemies visible and invisible, and make me a partaker of the heavenly kingdom.[^2871]

*Canon 2, Ode 7, Theotokion:* O Virgin Theotokos: entreat thy Son, together with the Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs, to deliver us, who ever honor thee, from future distress.[^2872]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Unto God Who is unceasingly glorified by Angels in the highest, the heavens of heavens, the earth, and the mountains, and the hills, and the deep, and the whole human race: with hymns bless Him as Creator and Redeemer, and exalt Him above all for ever.*[^2873]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 8, Troparion 1:* Because I have not had Thy fear dwelling in my heart, I, devoid of conscience, have fulfilled all carnal pleasure, and I tremble at Thy judgment, O King of all! Abhor me not who now repent.[^2874]

*Canon 1, Ode 8, Troparion 2:* Vouchsafe me, O Plenteous in mercy, to pass over into the holy land wherein the meek dwell, washing me by repentance from earthly sin, O Thou Who wast born sinlessly upon earth of the Virgin.[^2875]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 1, Ode 8, Martyrikon 1:* In your own blood were your feet dyed crimson, tripping up the enemy, and righteously accomplishing your journey to the heavens, O all-glorious passion-bearers of Christ, the God of all.[^2876]

*Canon 1, Ode 8, Martyrikon 2:* Having stripped yourselves for sufferings and great struggles with eagerness, ye exposed the enemy and clothed him in shame. Therefore, wearing crowns in the heavens, ye dance for joy, O glorious passion-bearers.[^2877]

*Canon 1, Ode 8, Theotokion:* Having loved thee as the beauty of Jacob, the Lord Who is fair in beauty, entering into thy womb, O All-immaculate One, illumined human nature with beautifying gifts surpassing understanding.[^2878]

*Another Heirmos:*
*United with unendurable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remained unharmed by the flame and sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all for ever.*[^2879]

*Refrain: Holy Forerunner and Baptist of the Savior John, pray to God for us.*
*Canon 2, Ode 8, Troparion 1:* Thou didst proclaim the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of men, O divine Forerunner John! Pray unto Him to loose the burden of my sins, and make me worthy of the portion of the saved.[^2880]

*Canon 2, Ode 8, Troparion 2:* Deliver me from the flame of the burning furnace, and from outer unillumined darkness, who am wholly held fast in the darkness of evil deeds, I pray Thee, O Word of God, supremely unoriginate, for the sake of Thy glorious and divine Baptist.[^2881]

*Canon 2, Ode 8, Troparion 3:* O divine Prophet of the Lord, who didst preach fruitful repentance unto empty and barren souls: cleanse my thorn-bearing soul from all pleasures, that I may bring forth ears of good deeds.[^2882]

*Canon 2, Ode 8, Theotokion:* As Mother of God, pray together with the holy Angels and Prophets, the Apostles, and the Martyrs, that those who ever confess thee as Theotokos may be delivered from perils and afflictions, and all future torments.[^2883]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Blessed is the Lord God of Israel, Who has raised up a horn of salvation for us in the house of His servant David, through the tender mercy of His loving-kindness, whereby the Dayspring from on high has visited us, and has guided us into the way of peace.*[^2884]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Canon 1, Ode 9, Troparion 1:* Behold, now is the acceptable time, and the day of purification! Desire at last to turn back, O soul, and produce fruits of repentance, lest the dread axe of death find thee barren, and, cutting thee down like the barren fig tree of old, send thee unto the fire yonder.[^2885]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Canon 1, Ode 9, Troparion 2:* Even as the rich man once rejoiced in delights, having much mercilessness toward his neighbor, and feared not the unquenchable fire: therefore, O Master, soften for me the petrification of my soul, that at last, having been illumined by mercy, I who am darkened may be saved.[^2886]

*Canon 1, Ode 9, Martyrikon 1:* Marked, O holy Martyrs, with the divine blood of Christ, and suffering firmly with faith by miracles, ye cast down the faithless enemies, and from foul delusion snatched away many people, having illumined them with the light of divine knowledge through divine transformations.[^2887]

*Canon 1, Ode 9, Martyrikon 2:* Ye appeared as sharpened swords, cutting down the enemy's regiments, O divine Martyrs of Christ, and vessels containing the radiant dawn of the Holy Trinity, and lamps shining the light of piety upon the faithful, and true soldiers of spiritual Sion.[^2888]

*Canon 1, Ode 9, Theotokion:* The Prophet foresaw thee as a light-bearing cloud, from which the great Sun, Christ God, appeared unto us, and illumined those who were formerly darkened. Him do thou entreat, O Gracious Lady, to drive away the clouds of my passions, and to illumine me with divine light.[^2889]

*Another Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and the letter of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: Every male that opens the womb is holy to God. Therefore the firstborn Word of the unoriginate Father, the Son Who is the firstborn of an unwedded Mother, do we magnify.*[^2890]

*Canon 2, Ode 9, Troparion 1:* Stooping down into the legal shadow and seeing the wealth of the radiant dawn of divine grace, thou dost illumine the ends of the earth, O Wise One, and dost drive away the darkness of foolishness, O Prophet; therefore do we honor thee.[^2891]

*Canon 2, Ode 9, Troparion 2:* As a Martyr of Christ, as the divine Baptist, as the lamp of repentance, and as the morning of piety, mediator between the Old and the New: having renewed with divine understanding my humble soul, grown old in malice, do thou illumine it.[^2892]

*Canon 2, Ode 9, Troparion 3:* In the fearful hour, in the dread hour, in the hour of judgment, deliver me, O Wise One, who am condemned, from the threat that awaits me there, having Him Who hears thy supplications, as the Friend of the Bridegroom, the Savior of our souls.[^2893]

*Canon 2, Ode 9, Theotokion:* As the Mother of God, as the Mother of the Word of God born of thee according to the flesh, O Pure One...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...with the Bodiless Powers, the Apostles and the Prophets, with the Hierarchs and the Martyrs, Him do thou ever entreat to give peace unto the world, O Most Pure Virgin Mother.[^2894]

*Then: It is truly meet... Litany, and Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^2895]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Compunction, Tone 3:*
Gather my scattered mind, O Lord, and cleanse my frozen heart, giving me repentance as unto Peter, sighing as unto the publican, and tears as unto the harlot, that with a loud voice I may cry unto Thee: O God, save me, as Thou alone art compassionate and the Lover of mankind.[^2896]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced... And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^2897]
Often singing hymns, I am found committing sins; with my tongue indeed uttering songs, but in my soul conceiving unseemly thoughts; but correct both, O Christ God, through repentance, and have mercy on me.[^2898]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^2899]
*Martyrikon:* The warriors of Christ cast away the fear of kings and torturers, and with good boldness and courageously confessed Him, the Lord of all, God and our King, and they pray for our souls.[^2900]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Without seed thou didst conceive by the Holy Spirit, and glorifying we sing unto thee: Rejoice, O Most Holy Virgin.[^2901]

*It is good to give praise... Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion and litany. The First Hour and dismissal.*[^2902]

*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 3:*
Thou didst cast out of Paradise, O Christ, our forefather Adam who set aside Thy commandment; but the thief, O Compassionate One, who confessed Thee upon the Cross, Thou didst settle therein, as he cried: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy kingdom.[^2903]

Defiled by the pleasures of life have I acquired my soul; unto Thy compassions do I come, being wholly desperate, and diligently do I cry unto Thee, O Christ, Who alone knowest secrets: Cleanse me by Thy mercy, O Lord.[^2904]

*Troparion 3:* Having been mediator between the Old and the New, O divine Baptist...

*On Tuesday at the Liturgy, Tone 3.*

...having renewed me who am grown old through transgressions, grant me by thy supplications, O All-praised One, to walk without stumbling along the paths of repentance, leading well into the kingdom of Christ.[^2905]

*Martyrikon:* Having fought the good fight, O noble sufferers, ye endured countless multitudes of afflictions, and therefore do ye ever alleviate the sicknesses of all, and drive away the harms of the spirits; therefore with faith do we glorify you, O holy ones.[^2906]

*Glory:* Light, and Life, and Creator of all, is the three-hypostatic Unity as It truly is, Which we glorify; for the Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, the Unity holding all things, Sovereign Master and Lord, one God in three Persons is known.[^2907]

*Both now:* Continually sinning and provoking the good God, O Virgin Mother, have compassion on me, and as thou art good, establish me now in the ways of repentance, that having escaped the torments to come, I may diligently sing of thy mercy, O Maiden.[^2908]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^2909]

*On Tuesday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 3.*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Great is the power of Thy martyrs" (Ве́лїя мꙋ́ченикъ):*[^2910]

*Sticheron 1:* Creation was sanctified, O Thou Who alone art longsuffering, by the precious blood that dripped from Thy ribs; and the rivers of polytheism were dried up, and Adam was delivered from condemnation by Thy crucifixion, O Word.[^2911]

*Sticheron 2:* He Who is the Creator of all and the Lord, and the God of all, becomes impoverished willingly; He endures suffering for mankind, being lifted up upon the Cross under the curse of the Law.[^2912]

*Sticheron 3:* By Thine own will art Thou crucified, O Benefactor; and having been pierced in Thy ribs with a spear, O Giver of life, Thou didst pour forth two fountains of immortality. Therefore unceasingly do we all faithfully glorify Thy sufferings, O Compassionate One.[^2913]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos, the same Tone:*
*Sticheron 1:* Beholding Him Who was born of thee hanging upon the Tree, O All-immaculate One, thou didst cry aloud, weeping: O my most beloved child...

*On Tuesday Evening, Tone 3.*

...child! Whither has Thy light-bearing beauty departed, which adorned human nature?[^2914]

*Sticheron 2:* Wounded by the weapon of the serpent, the beginner of evil, and lying half-dead upon the bed of despair, O Maiden, quickly heal me by the medicine of thy prayer; and save me, O my hope and protection.[^2915]

*Sticheron 3:* Lay aside, O my humble soul, the intolerable burdens of malice, and draw near weeping and crying aloud: Make me worthy, O Pure Virgin, to bear the light yoke of thy Son and God.[^2916]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* A sword pierced thy heart, O Most Pure One, when thou didst behold thy Son upon the Cross, and thou didst cry aloud: Show me not childless, O my Son and my God, Who didst preserve me a Virgin after childbirth.[^2917]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon.*
*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 3:*
I worship Thy precious Cross, O Christ, the guardian of the world, the salvation of us sinners, the great mercy seat, the victory of kings, the boast of the whole universe.[^2918]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... Even so our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^2919]
Moses, prefiguring the Cross upon the mountain, lifting up his hands on high, conquered Amalek; but Thou, O Savior, having stretched forth Thy palms upon the precious Cross, embracing me didst save me from the enemy's bondage, and hast given me the life-giving sign, to flee from the bow of them that oppose me. Therefore, O Word, do I worship Thy precious Cross.[^2920]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^2921]
*Martyrikon:* The Prophets, and Apostles of Christ, and Martyrs taught us to sing of the consubstantial Trinity, and they illumined the erring nations, and made the sons of men partakers with the Angels.[^2922]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When thou didst behold Him Who shone forth from thy womb setting upon the Cross, the never-setting Sun, and darkening the rays of the sun, thou didst cry aloud, weighed down in thy soul by a darkness of sorrows: O Thou Who didst set willingly, shine forth again, unto mine illumination and that of the whole world.[^2923]

*Then: Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart... Trisagion. After Our Father: troparia, litany, and dismissal.*[^2924]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 3.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 3.*

*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old gathered the waters into one gathering by His divine nod, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; to Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.*[^2925]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee, the Mother of God, as the fountain of compassion and mercy, I approach and pray to thy goodness, to grant me compunction, that I may mourn and weep for my transgressions, O All-pure Lady.[^2926]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me a drop of tears of the soul, washing away all the defilement of my deeds and evil thoughts, and cleansing the impurity of my soul, and making me a temple of the Holy Spirit of God.[^2927]

*Glory:* Tempest-tossed by tumult and the surging of my transgressions, and ever grievously overwhelmed by the works of the adversary, and now cast down into the depth of perdition, I cry unto thee, O All-immaculate One: Save me.[^2928]

*Both now:* For the sake of the mercy of thy loving-kindness, O Sovereign Lady of all, have mercy on my wretched soul, and deliver me from the everlasting fire and demonic assault, who now flee under thy protection, O Theotokos.[^2929]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who didst bring all things out of nothingness, creating them by the Word and perfecting them by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.*[^2930]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* With divine light, O All-praised One, illumine my mind darkened by evil thoughts; for thou gavest birth to the Light, the co-eternal One Who shone forth from the Father.[^2931]

*Troparion 2:* Tripping up the steps of my soul, the enemy of the righteous dashed me to the ground; but by thy right hand, O Pure Lady, raise me up.[^2932]

*Glory:* With the voices of the publican, wretched that I am, I cry unto thee: Cleanse me, O Lady, and by thy prayer grant remission of transgressions unto thy servant.[^2933]

*Both now:* Heal the wounds of my soul, and quiet the tempestuous surge of thoughts, O Lady, and grant me a peaceful disposition.[^2934]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown firm love toward us, O Lord; for Thou gavest Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore we cry unto Thee with thanksgiving: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^2935]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* I have defiled my flesh with pleasures and sensuality, and with foul thoughts have I stained the purity of my soul, and I have darkened my mind; despise me not, therefore, O Lady, thy servant.[^2936]

*Troparion 2:* Be thou my deliverance, and refuge, and might, and horn of salvation, O Pure One, and protectress, ever delivering me from every affliction, and putting to shame all mine enemies.[^2937]

*Glory:* Held fast now by many misfortunes, wretch that I am, and ever plunged into demonic deceits, I flee unto thee now: by thy fervent prayer save me, thy servant.[^2938]

*Both now:* A dark night of passions holds me fast, wretched that I am; but with thy light, O Gracious Lady, scatter the clouds of my soul, and guide me unto the light of God's commandments, O All-immaculate One.[^2939]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*As Isaiah in a figure beheld God lifted up upon a throne, borne aloft by the Angels of glory, "O wretched am I," cried he; "for I have foreseen God incarnate, the never-setting Light, and Him Who rules over the world."*[^2940]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The time of my life has vanished like smoke, and I have drawn nigh unto the gates of death, and I am terrified of demonic assault; for their deeds have I ever done. O All-immaculate One, have compassion and save me.[^2941]

*Troparion 2:* Drying up the abyss of my evils, O Virgin, grant me rivers of tears, and extinguish the whole flame of my passions, O Maiden, and vouchsafe me to be delivered from the fire and other torments in the day of judgment.[^2942]

*Glory:* Heal my soul that is sick with sins, O Most Pure One, by thy tender mercy, and vouchsafe me ever to perform the commandments of thy Son in humility, that I may receive His goodness.[^2943]

*Both now: Stavrotheotokion:* Bereaved, O my Son, of bridal raiment is Thy divine Church, clothed with the holy blood from Thy ribs; but I bear mournful pains, beholding Thy sufferings upon the Cross, the Mother of the Word weeping cried aloud.[^2944]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The uttermost abyss of sins has compassed me, and my spirit fails; but stretch...*

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...stretch forth Thy high arm, O Master, and as Peter, O Helmsman, save me.*[^2945]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The Son of God showed thee to be a great refuge unto sinners, being incarnate of thy pure blood, O Bride of God; therefore be merciful unto thy servants.[^2946]

*Troparion 2:* Illumine with thy radiant dawn, O Gracious Lady, the darkened eyes of my heart with improper thoughts, and make me a child of the light, and settle me in a place of light.[^2947]

*Glory:* The waves of passionate thoughts ever trouble me, O Most Pure One, and the tempest of deceitful spirits overwhelms me; but establish me upon the rock of dispassion.[^2948]

*Both now:* I have fallen asleep unto the death of the soul, and I lie in the grave of despair; but give me a hand, and raise me up, I pray thee, guiding me unto the life of repentance.[^2949]

*Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3:*
Having acquired the Cross of thy Son as a rod of strength, O Theotokos, thereby do we cast down the insolence of the enemies, unceasingly magnifying thee with love.[^2950]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Of old, the three youths bowed not down to the golden image, the Persian idol, singing in the midst of the furnace: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.*[^2951]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Gird my soul that is enfeebled by sins with strength, O Pure One, and save me who cry aloud unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^2952]

*Troparion 2:* Fearing the hour of death, on account of immortal and eternal torment, I fall down before thee: Save me, O Maiden, from the snare of the hunters, O Birthgiver of God.[^2953]

*Glory:* O thou who didst contain the uncontainable God in thy womb: my mind is constrained by many transgressions, O Theotokos; deliver it from their condemnation.[^2954]

*Both now:* The human race prays to thee, O Theotokos and Lady: have mercy on thy servants who with faith cry aloud unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^2955]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*United with unendurable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remained unharmed by the flame and sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all for ever.*[^2956]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 3.*

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Having truly envied the prodigal, and having consumed my whole life in sins, now I cry: I have sinned against thee, make me, O Maiden, as one of the hired servants of thy Son and Creator, that I may glorify thee unto all ages.[^2957]

*Troparion 2:* My soul is filled with evils, and I am numbered with all them that go down into the pit; but do thou, O Virgin Theotokos, lead me up out of the pit of passions and from the mire of the clay of my evils.[^2958]

*Glory:* Pray unto Christ, whom thou didst bear from thy holy loins, O Theotokos, to grant me forgiveness of my many transgressions, that I may sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2959]

*Both now:* When by the commandment of God my soul is about to depart from this life, snatch me away from the snare of the hunters, O Most Pure One, who cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^2960]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and the letter of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: Every male that opens the womb is holy to God. Therefore the firstborn Word of the unoriginate Father, the Son Who is the firstborn of an unwedded Mother, do we magnify.*[^2961]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* The human race, O All-immaculate One, enriched now by thine invincible prayer, cries aloud day and night: Cease not ever to pray to the Creator and thy Son, to have compassion on those who sing of thee.[^2962]

*Troparion 2:* The arrows of improper passions have wounded my soul through demonic assaults, and with hostile arrows of thoughts they ever trouble my mind; therefore heal, O Maiden, mine incurable wounds.[^2963]

*Glory:* Take away quickly from me, O Pure Bride of God, the wounds of the warring enemies, for their uprising and great malice and arrogance I, wretch that I am, can no longer endure; but hasten to deliver me.[^2964]

*Both now:* Thou knowest the weakness of my humble body, the wounds of my soul, the groaning of my heart, and the wandering and deception of my mind; therefore by thy mercy grant healing unto both.[^2965]

*On Wednesday at Matins.*
*After the 1st Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3:*
The Cross was set up on earth, and touched heaven; not that the wood...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...reached unto the height, but because Thou upon it dost fill all things; O Lord, glory to Thee.[^2966]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^2967]
Upon the cypress, and the pine, and the cedar, wast Thou lifted up, O Lamb of God, that Thou mightest save them that with faith worship Thy voluntary crucifixion; O Christ God, glory to Thee.[^2968]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Having acquired the Cross of thy Son as a rod of strength, O Theotokos, thereby do we cast down the insolence of the enemies, unceasingly magnifying thee with love.[^2969]

*After the 2nd Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3:*
Having willed to suffer the Cross and death, in the midst of creation didst Thou set it up; when Thou wast well-pleased, O Savior, to have Thy body nailed: then also the sun hid its rays; then also the thief, beholding these things, singing of Thee upon the cross, cried reverently: Remember me, O Lord! and believing, received Paradise.[^2970]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He has wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^2971]
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^2972]
At Thine unsearchable power and voluntary crucifixion, the angelic hosts were amazed, beholding how the Invisible One was wounded in the flesh, willing to deliver mankind from corruption; therefore, as unto the Giver of life, we cry unto Thee: Glory, O Christ, to Thy tender compassions.[^2973]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^2974]
*Martyrikon:* Armed with the whole armor of Christ, and clad in the weapon of faith, ye cast down the hostile regiments through your sufferings; for eagerly, with the hope of life, ye endured all the threats and wounds of the torturers; therefore also ye received crowns, O patient-minded Martyrs of Christ.[^2975]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* A reproachful death and crucifixion didst Thou willingly endure, O Compassionate One; beholding which, She that bare Thee was wounded. By Her supplications, for the tender compassion of Thy mercy, O Thou Who alone art supremely good and the Lover of mankind, O Lord, have compassion and save the world, Thou Who takest away the sins of the world.[^2976]

*After the 3rd Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3:*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Beholding the beauty of thy virginity" (Красотѣ̀ дѣ́вства):*[^2977]
Having accepted crucifixion and death, Thou didst pour forth immortal life for us, O Christ Savior, and didst deliver the world from corruption; therefore do we glorify Thy saving passions, O Giver of life, Lover...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...Lover of mankind, whereby we are all saved, having the invincible weapon of peace, Thy precious Cross.[^2978]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Thou didst endure the Cross dishonorably, O Master, Who art higher than all creation, that Thou mightest honor me who was formerly grievously dishonored; and in Thy ribs wast Thou pierced with a spear, O Longsuffering One, willing to deliver me, Thy creation, from corruption. I sing of Thy great tender compassion and goodness, O Lover of mankind.[^2979]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* The Unwedded, Pure One and Thy Mother, O Christ, beholding Thee hanging dead upon the Cross, wept maternally and said: What has the lawless and ungrateful Jewish assembly rendered unto Thee, which enjoyed Thy many and great gifts, O my Son? I sing of Thy divine condescension.[^2980]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Sicknesses hast Thou stilled for men by Thy sicknesses, O Word.)*[^2981]
*A Work of Joseph.*[^2982]
*Tone 3, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old gathered the waters into one gathering by His divine nod, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; to Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.*[^2983]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* Many-painful passions hast Thou stilled, O Word, by the passions of Thy precious flesh, and hast saved men whom the adversary wounded of old, who piously worship Thine inscrutable dispensation.[^2984]

*Troparion 2:* He who bound man in Paradise through deceit, by the transgression of the commandment, is bound with indissoluble bonds, O Lord, wherewith incarnate Thou wast bound willingly, loosing our transgressions, O Lover of mankind.[^2985]

*Martyrikon 1:* Beholding creation endangered by drowning through the delusions of demons, the passion-bearers appeared as peaceful harbors unto the faithful, having drowned all the power of the proud one in streams of blood, O renowned ones.[^2986]

*Martyrikon 2:* The choir of martyrs, brightly united with the heavenly company, is ever illumined with the splendors of the uncreated Godhead, and illumines those on earth who glorify their miracles with faith.[^2987]

*Theotokion:* Higher than the heavenly ranks didst thou appear, having given birth on earth to God the Word, O Most Pure One, Who by His passions has raised us unto heavenly things...[^2988]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...and by the Precious Cross, for the sake of His goodness.

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 3. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* Grant the furrows of my soul to be richly watered with tears, O Virgin, and make me worthy to bring forth fruit an hundredfold, O Lady, and fill my heart with gladness, that I may glorify thee.[^2989]

*Troparion 2:* For the sake of the tender compassion of thy mercy, O Lady of all, have mercy on my wretched soul, and deliver me from the everlasting fire and from demonic assault, who flee now beneath thy protection, O Theotokos.[^2990]

*Troparion 3:* As the undefiled temple of the King, my heart which is defiled by improper passions, I pray thee, O Most Pure One, cleanse by thy supplications, and enrich with the deeds of a pure life, O thou who art full of divine grace.[^2991]

*Theotokion:* Why passest thou thy life in slothfulness, O passionate soul, and fearest not the day of the dread judgment? But arise henceforth from the despondency of sins, and cry aloud now fervently and warmly: Save me, O pure Lady.[^2992]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who from nothingness broughtest all things into being, which are created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.*[^2993]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* Thee Who in the nature of Thy Godhead art passionless, yet becamest passionate in the flesh for the sake of goodness, did the Hebrew assembly put to death upon the Tree, Thee Who dost make us immortal.[^2994]

*Troparion 2:* O Word, Who hangedst the earth upon the waters, Thou wast willingly hanged upon the Tree, raising me up unto the heavens, who had fallen into the pit of malice.[^2995]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^2996]
*Martyrikon 1:* Adorned with wounds, O all-praised martyrs of Christ, ye stand before the Lord, receiving recompense from Him with a most rich hand.[^2997]

*Martyrikon 2:* With a joyful soul did the martyrs hasten unto wounds, working utter grief for the serpent, and filling the angelic choirs with joy.[^2998]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Christ lifted up upon the Cross, the Unwedded One, weeping, said: Thou hast set from mine eyes, O Sun of glory, Thou Who dost enlighten those in darkness.[^2999]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Ode 3. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* Deliver from Gehenna and all other torments in the hour of judgment thy servant...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...O All-immaculate One, and make him a partaker of the kingdom of thy Son and God.[^3000]

*Troparion 2:* Heal the wounds of my soul, and still the tempestuous turmoil of my thoughts, O Lady, and grant me a peaceful disposition.[^3001]

*Troparion 3:* Being now grievously inflamed by passions, there is no healing for my soul, O All-immaculate One; yet leave me not utterly, thy servant, O Virgin.[^3002]

*Theotokion:* Dead in soul through the multitude of the malice of passions, make me alive, O Lady, who didst ineffably give birth to Christ the Giver of life.[^3003]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thou gavest Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore with thanksgiving we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3004]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* Contempt indeed didst Thou endure, O Christ Master, Who art higher than all honor, being struck upon the head with a reed, that Thou mightest honor me who was dishonored by transgression, O Lover of mankind.[^3005]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast willingly crowned with a crown of thorns, O Longsuffering One, as the true King, and the thorn-bearing ancestral sin Thou didst eradicate from the root. I sing praises unto Thy sufferings, O Savior.[^3006]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3007]
