*Martyrikon 1:* Unbroken by the torments of wounds did ye remain, O Martyrs, crushing the enemy's delusion, and trampling under your feet him who boasted immeasurably and was utterly withered by folly.[^3008]

*Martyrikon 2:* With corruptible bodies ye acquired divine incorruption, through passions steadfastly imitating the precious Passion of the Passionless One, O all-praised Martyrs, numbered with all the bodiless hosts.[^3009]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* The Prophet of old beheld thee as a scroll, O Maiden, in which by the Father's finger the incarnate Word was written, Who with a spear tore up the ancestral handwriting of debt, O Most Pure One.[^3010]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Ode 4. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* When in the night the Bridegroom shall come to judge the earth, then with a bright can-...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...-dle, vouchsafe me to go forth to meet Him, O Most Pure One, and to worship His coming.[^3011]

*Troparion 2:* The unilluminated night of passions holds fast thy servant; but by thy light, O Good One, disperse the cloud of my soul, and guide me unto the light of the divine commandments, O All-immaculate One.[^3012]

*Troparion 3:* By a tempest of temptations and by the surge of evil thoughts am I wholly overwhelmed, and ever dragged down into the abyss of despair. Save thy servant, O Lady, I cry unto thee.[^3013]

*Theotokion:* When my soul is about to be separated from the flesh, then stand by me mercifully, and snatch me away from the assault of demons who unceasingly seek my destruction, O Maiden.[^3014]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Upon earth Thou, the Invisible One, didst appear, and willingly didst dwell among men, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early unto Thee, we hymn Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^3015]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* As a lamb wast Thou lifted up upon the Tree, and offered Thyself as a sacrifice to the Father for us, O Good One, and didst abolish idolatrous sacrifices, O Almighty One.[^3016]

*Troparion 2:* Pierced in the side with a spear, O Giver of life, Thou didst pour forth two fountains of salvation unto those who proclaim Thee as One of the Trinity, bearing two energies.[^3017]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3018]
*Martyrikon 1:* Finding Thee by faith as a strong foundation and an immovable rock, O Jesus, the valiant passion-bearers built themselves up as precious stones.[^3019]

*Martyrikon 2:* Strengthened by the power of God through faith, ye brought into subjection all the cruel power of the deceiver, O passion-bearing martyrs, and were radiantly crowned.[^3020]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Having incorruptibly given birth to Christ, thou didst recreate our nature which was previously corrupted through transgression, O Most Holy Lady, protectress of our souls.[^3021]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Ode 5. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* Behold, O Pure One, the affliction of my humble and passionate soul; behold also the assaults of bodiless foes, and deliver me from their harm.[^3022]

*Troparion 2:* When my mind through demonic temptations is ever drawn away into improper thoughts, leave me not, O Lady, but establish me immovably.[^3023]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 3:* Eating with sinners did thy Son appear, for the sake of His great mercy; having been likened unto them, O Pure One, have mercy now on me who have sinned more than all.[^3024]

*Theotokion:* Let me not be rebuked by the fury of thy Son at the time of His coming, nor let me be chastised by His wrath, O All-hymned One, but save me by thy supplications.[^3025]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The lowest abyss of sins has surrounded me, and my spirit fails; but stretching forth Thy lofty arm, O Master, save me, O Helmsman, even as Peter.*[^3026]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* The serpent led me out of Eden, that deceiver who deceived me by deceptive food; but Christ, being willingly lifted up upon the Tree, grants me again mine ancient entrance.[^3027]

*Troparion 2:* He who wounded us was wounded and remained incurably hurt by Thy wounded side, O Benefactor and Lord; but we the faithful have been healed by Thy wounds, wherewith Thou wast willingly wounded.[^3028]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3029]
*Martyrikon 1:* Ye appeared in the midst of the fire, O all-wise soldiers of Christ, roasted as lambs and brought unto the banquet of God the King of all, inheriting ineffable gladness.[^3030]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye dispense rivers of healings, drawing from inexhaustible treasures, and ye dry up the streams of passions, O passion-bearers, watering the assemblies of the faithful.[^3031]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding the death of the Word Who was incarnate from thy blood, O All-immaculate One, maternally didst thou cry aloud and magnify Him Who is the Cause of life, O Mother, Virgin, Lady.[^3032]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Ode 6. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* By the waves of this life to my soul's hurt am I, wretched one, ever overwhelmed, and by the winds of the enemy am I ever tossed; now cry I unto thee: O Theotokos, help me.[^3033]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me streams of spiritual tears, O most immaculate Maiden, that I may wash away the mire of my transgressions, the tumult of passions, and bodily defilements.[^3034]

*Troparion 3:* I have fallen asleep in the death of the soul, and lie in the grave of despair; but stretch forth a hand unto me, and raise me up, I pray thee, guiding me unto the repentance of life.[^3035]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Theotokion:* Dry up the turbid rivers of mine evil deeds, O Mother of God, with streams of compunction, and direct me unto the waters of relief on the day of judgment.[^3036]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Unto the golden image of old, the Persian worship, the three youths bowed not down, singing in the midst of the furnace: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.*[^3037]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* By Thy sufferings Thou didst abolish our sicknesses, O Lover of mankind, and now hast brought unto painless life those who piously worship Thy precious Passion, O God of all.[^3038]

*Troparion 2:* When creation beheld Thee crucified, O Christ, it was altered and trembled; the earth shook, the rocks were rent, and the course of the sun hid its light.[^3039]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3040]
*Martyrikon 1:* Submitting unto the law of Christ, the martyrs turned away from the blandishments of the lawless; and lawfully contending in the midst of the tribunal, they were gloriously crowned.[^3041]

*Martyrikon 2:* Ye were not burned by fire, having acquired a purpose warmer than fire, O crown-bearing passion-bearers of the Lord, crying: Blessed art Thou, O God.[^3042]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Thou didst stand beholding Christ lifted up upon the Cross, Whom thou didst bear, O Most Pure One, and didst cry: Leave me not childless, Whom Thou didst preserve pure even after childbirth.[^3043]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Ode 7. Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* As merciful Mother of God, vouchsafe thy mercy unto thy servants, O Lady, who with faith cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3044]

*Troparion 2:* Consume the pasture of my sins and vile deeds, O Pure One, that with faith I may cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3045]

*Troparion 3:* The human race entreats thee, O Theotokos and Lady: Have mercy on thy servants, who with faith cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3046]

*Theotokion:* At the dread tribunal of Christ, when I am to be judged as guilty, O Most Pure One, appear unto me, delivering me from every torment by thy warm intercession.[^3047]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The Babylonian furnace burned not the youths, nor did the fire of the Godhead consume the Virgin; therefore...*

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*...with the youths let us the faithful cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^3048]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* When Thou wast crucified, Paradise was opened again, and the sword that turned against us turned its back, put to shame by the spear that pierced Thy holy side, O most merciful Christ.[^3049]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy spear was the adversary wounded and cast down; and fallen Adam returns unto life, crying unto Thee Who wast willingly slain, O Christ: Blessing Thee, I glorify Thee, O my most merciful God.[^3050]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3051]
*Martyrikon 1:* The world is enlightened by your roots and excellences, O sufferers, and by your innumerable miracles, and is delivered from the darkness of afflictions, crying in faith: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^3052]

*Martyrikon 2:* An object of mockery is seen lying beneath your feet, O Saints, he who before boasted to destroy the earth and sea; but Christ adorns you with His life-bearing right hand with unfading crowns, O ever-renowned ones.[^3053]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* In time didst thou bring forth Him Who is above times, Who loosens temporal bonds by His own bonds, O Most Pure One, even Adam the first-formed, and binds him with the bonds of His sweet love.[^3054]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 3, Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Joined unto the unbearable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remained unhurt by the flame and sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all for ever.*[^3055]

*Troparion 1:* Cease not, O Pure One, to pray that those who honor thee in compunction and cry unto thy Son may be delivered from the snares of the devil: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^3056]

*Troparion 2:* When by the commandment of God my soul is about to depart from this life, snatch me away from the snares of those who hunt for me, O Theotokos, who sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^3057]

*Troparion 3:* An unilluminated night holds me fast, for I have enslaved myself to dark deeds; but do thou, who art the gates of light, illumine me with thy light, who sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all for ever.[^3058]

*Troparion 4:* Grant thy mercy, O Lady of the world, unto thy servant, I pray thee...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...and from the expected condemnation snatch away thy people, O All-immaculate One, that with thanksgiving we may cry unto thee: Glory to thee, O Queen of all.[^3059]

*Canon of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*Tone 3, Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Her who was prefigured in Sinai unto the lawgiver Moses by the bush and the fire, and who in her womb unburnt conceived the divine fire, the all-radiant and inextinguishable lamp, the true Theotokos, honoring her in hymns do we magnify.*[^3060]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*
*Troparion 1:* That Thou mightest find the drachma which was lost, O Christ, Thou didst kindle Thy flesh upon the Cross, O Good One, and makest Thy heavenly hosts partakers of joy, O Giver of life. Singing with them unto Thee as our Benefactor, in hymns do we magnify Thee.[^3061]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou didst lift up Thy hands upon the Cross, O Christ, Thou didst strengthen by Thy power my hands that were previously weakened by many passions, and didst establish the knees that were truly feeble for running the divine race. Therefore do we magnify Thee.[^3062]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3063]
*Martyrikon 1:* Burning in the flame and beneath immeasurable wounds, Thy steadfast and marvelous sufferers found Thee, O Christ, as the dew of refreshment. Therefore rejoicing they walked the path in their desire for honors, unceasingly magnifying Thee in hymns.[^3064]

*Martyrikon 2:* The multitude of sufferers, the choir of saints, entreats Thee, O Christ, for the congregation of people who have provoked Thee much; by the multitude of Thy mercy, O Compassionate One, cleanse the multitude of our iniquities, as the Lover of mankind.[^3065]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Bearing the likeness of earthly beings, thou didst give birth to the Brightness of the Father, O Virgin. Beholding Him lifted up upon the Cross, the sun could not bear to look upon Him and was darkened, and the darkness of idolatry was diminished; therefore with Him do we magnify thee.[^3066]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 3, Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and the letter of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: Every male that opens the womb is holy to God. Therefore the firstborn Word of the unoriginate Father, the Son Who is the firstborn of an unwedded Mother, do we magnify.*[^3067]

*Troparion 1:* The end draws near, O my soul, judgment is at the doors; turn away from shameful deeds, and lay hold on the good life; for thou hast a helper, the Theotokos, who delivers thee from every affliction.[^3068]

*Troparion 2:* Among noetic thieves have I fallen, wretch that I am, and through their wiles have I been stripped of my garment of virtues to my soul's hurt; and filled with soul-corrupting passions...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...I cry unto thee: O All-immaculate Lady, despise me not.[^3069]

*Troparion 3:* All my life, O Most Pure One, is spent in despondency, and by despair have I drawn near unto hades now. But grant me amendment, O Pure One, and leave me not to be afflicted therein, who flee unto thy protection.[^3070]

*Theotokion:* Thou knowest the weakness of my humble body, the wounds of my soul, the groaning of my heart, and the wandering and delusion of my mind. Therefore by thy tender compassion grant healing unto both.[^3071]

*Then: It is truly meet: Litany and the rest.*[^3072]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 3:*
*Sticheron 1:* The tree of disobedience put forth death for the world; but the Tree of the Cross put forth life and incorruption. Therefore we pray Thee, the crucified Lord, that the light of Thy countenance may be signed upon us.[^3073]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: [and look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works,] and guide Thou their sons.*[^3074]

*Sticheron 2:* Through envy of sweet pleasure was I cast out, having fallen with a grievous fall; yet Thou didst not despise me, O Master, but having accepted for my sake the crucifixion which belonged to me, Thou savest me and leadest me into glory; O my Redeemer, glory to Thee.[^3075]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^3076]

*Martyrikon:* The powers of the holy Angels were astonished at the suffering of the martyrs, how, though clad in flesh, they disregarded torments, having become imitators of the passions of Christ the Savior; and they pray for our souls.[^3077]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* When the all-hymned Ewe-lamb, the Virgin Thy Mother, beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O my Christ, sobbing with weeping she cried aloud: O my Son and my God, leave me not childless, whom Thou didst preserve pure even after childbirth.[^3078]

*At the Liturgy, Beatitudes, Tone 3:*
*Troparion 1:* Adam the forefather, having cast aside Thy commandment, O Christ, Thou didst expel from Paradise; but the thief who confessed Thee upon the Cross, O Compassionate One, Thou didst settle therein, as he cried: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy kingdom.[^3079]

*On Wednesday at the Liturgy, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast inflamed for my sake, and pierced with a spear in Thy ribs, O my Jesus, Thou didst pour forth two saving fountains for me. Therefore, being saved by Thy passion, O Christ, I hymn and glorify Thy tender-heartedness, crying: Remember me in Thy kingdom.[^3080]

*Troparion 3:* Numbered with two lawless men, Thou tookest away the iniquities of us all, O Jesus, and crowned with thorns as King of all, Thou didst cut down from the root the thorns of the forefather's sin, O Christ; therefore with faith do we now glorify Thy Passion.[^3081]

*Martyrikon:* Ye who imitated the sufferings of Christ, O valiant sufferers, all-glorious martyrs, and cast down the enemy's delusion by divine power, have obtained heavenly glory: Pray for us all, O Holy Ones.[^3082]

*Glory:* O Tri-hypostatic Unity and indivisible, all-effecting Trinity, one Essence and Power! Cover Thy singers from every harm of the enemy, and make them worthy of Thy kingdom, which they that lived well have obtained.[^3083]

*Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding upon the Cross Him Whom thou didst enflesh from pure blood, weeping didst thou cry aloud, O Mother of God, Maiden: What is this, O Child, that the evil assembly has rendered unto Thee, putting Thee to death, Who art the Life and Resurrection of all the faithful?[^3084]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas[^3085]

*On Wednesday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 3.*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "Great is the power of Thy martyrs" (Ве́лїа мꙋ́ченикъ):*
*Sticheron 1:* By the prayers of Thy precious and divine Apostles, O generous Lover of mankind, Who alone art merciful, grant Thy peace unto Thy servants, and save from distresses those who hymn Thee and worship with faith.[^3086]

*Sticheron 2:* When ye shall sit upon twelve thrones with the Judge of all to judge every creature: show me uncondemned, and deliver me from darkness and every torment, O my benefactors, divine Apostles.[^3087]

*On Wednesday Evening, Tone 3.*

*Sticheron 3:* Deliver me from the arrows of the ungodly one, as eyewitnesses of God, quenching his wiles; and bedew me with spiritual dew, I pray you, O my wise benefactors, divine Apostles.[^3088]

*Then in the Menaion, Stichera to the Saint.*
*If there be no Menaion, we sing Stichera to the holy Wonderworker Nicholas. Tone and podoben the same:*[^3089]
*Sticheron 1:* Wounded am I, wretched one, by the biting of the serpent, and I lie cast down, dead and breathless; by thy vigilant supplication raise me up speedily, O all-blessed Hierarch, that I may glorify thy grace which is swift to hear.[^3090]

*Sticheron 2:* Foreseeing that my mind is darkened by evil thoughts and lies prostrate, the crafty one in no wise ceases hunting after me. But as God, show compassion and save me, by the prayers of Nicholas.[^3091]

*Sticheron 3:* A great salvation hast thou appeared unto all of us, O all-blessed Nicholas; for thou dost deliver thy servants from all tribulations and distresses, and from temptations, sicknesses, adversities, and invisible enemies.[^3092]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:* Great is the power of thy wonders, O Pure One; for thou deliverest from tribulations, and savest from death, and snatchest away from unexpected calamities, and loosest sorrows, and takest away the transgressions of men.[^3093]

*O Gladsome Light: The Prokeimenon of the day.*[^3094]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 3:*
*Sticheron 1:* Into all the earth went out your sound, O holy Apostles; ye destroyed the delusion of idols, proclaiming the knowledge of God. O good is your contest, O Blessed Ones! Therefore we sing and glorify your memory.[^3095]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: [behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden unto the hands of her mistress,] so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us.*[^3096]

*Sticheron 2:* Being branches of the life-bearing Vine, O glorious Apostles, ye offered yourselves unto God as fruit of piety. Therefore, having boldness before Him, entreat that He grant peace and great mercy unto our souls.[^3097]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us: [for we are utterly filled with abasement;] our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^3098]

*Martyrikon:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, established by faith and strengthened by hope, spiritually united by the love of Thy Cross, destroyed the enemy's tyranny; and having obtained crowns, with the Bodiless Powers they pray for our souls.[^3099]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Snatch me away, who sin exceedingly, O Maiden, by thy great supplication from the inescapable flame; and direct me, O Pure One, by thy prayers, guiding me unto saving paths by thy maternal intercessions.[^3100]

*On Wednesday at Small Compline, Canon of Supplication to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 3, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by His divine nodding gathered the waters together into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; to Him alone do we sing, for He has been glorified.*[^3101]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Grant me sighing, O Theotokos, contrition and tears of the soul, that I may weep for my many transgressions and for the uncorrected state of my conscience, O Maiden, and I pray for thy tender compassion.[^3102]

*Troparion 2:* Slipping in my thoughts, I fall into the abyss of perdition. I pray now for thy protection, O Virgin: establish immovably the feebleness of my mind upon the firm rock of God's commandments.[^3103]

*Glory:* I bear the fig tree of fruitlessness, and I fear the cutting down and the burning, lest my Creator God send me into the fire. O Lady, anticipating this, make me fruitful with the works of repentance, that I may glorify thee.[^3104]

*Both now:* As the undefiled temple of the King, my heart which is defiled by improper passions, I pray thee, cleanse, O Most Pure One; and enrich it with chastity and a pure life and with good deeds, O thou who art full of divine grace.[^3105]

*Tone 3, Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who from nothingness broughtest all things into being, which are created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.*[^3106]

*Troparion 1:* Many are they that wage war against me and afflict me, and there is no respite to find nor deliverance from those that have surrounded me; yet despise not my supplication, O Pure One.[^3107]

*Troparion 2:* Weighed down by the multitude of evils and by my many transgressions, I cannot look up to the heavenly height; but do thou grant me forgiveness, O Most Pure One.[^3108]

*Glory:* To tear apart my humble soul the crafty serpent daily...

*On Wednesday at Small Compline, Tone 3.*

...attempts to do; but shatter, O Theotokos, his soul-destroying teeth.[^3109]

*Both now:* Deliver me, O Theotokos, thy servant, from every besetting distress and storm of sin, and direct me unto the saving haven by thy prayers.[^3110]

*Tone 3, Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thou gavest Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore with thanksgiving we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3111]

*Troparion 1:* I tremble when I consider the gulf of my sins and the fierce and great tempest of contrary thoughts; but do thou pilot me, and direct me unto tranquility, O Most Pure One.[^3112]

*Troparion 2:* Grievous perplexity has now overtaken my soul, senselessness and forgetfulness of the evils which I have committed in life; yet do thou, O Most Pure One, lead me unto repentance for the things wrought by me.[^3113]

*Glory:* Quench the fire of passions with streams of tears, and with the fire of the Spirit burn up the worldly turmoil and thorns that choke my mind, O Parent of God; for thou art my protection and praise.[^3114]

*Both now:* By the tumult of tribulations and tempests am I wholly overwhelmed through evil thoughts, and ever dragged down into the abyss of despair, I cry unto thee: O Lady, save me, thy servant.[^3115]

*Tone 3, Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*As Isaiah typically beheld God lifted up upon a throne, borne aloft by angels of glory: O wretched one that I am, he cried, for I have seen beforehand God incarnate, the Unwaning Light, Who rules with peace.*[^3116]

*Troparion 1:* Thou knowest the sickness of my soul and the feebleness of my flesh, O Lady, and the corruption and instability of my mind. Therefore vouchsafe me thy mercy and grace, that being saved by thy protection, I may glorify thee.[^3117]

*Troparion 2:* Shut not against me the tender compassion of thy compassions, O all-immaculate Lady, but grant thy mercies unto him that comes to thee in compunction, and relief and forgiveness of grievous things, that with thanksgiving I may sing thy marvelous deeds.[^3118]

*Glory:* Multiplied, O Virgin, are they that hate me without a cause and afflict me now, more than the hairs of my head, O Pure One, seeking to tear me apart and make food of me; therefore fill them with shame and turn them back.[^3119]

*On Wednesday at Small Compline, Tone 3.*

*Both now:* O thou who gavest peace to the world and salvation unto all, having given birth to the Divine Peace, O Virgin: through the fear and love of Christ the Savior, calm the warfare of the passions of my soul and body, O Pure One.[^3120]

*Tone 3, Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The lowest abyss of sins has surrounded me, and my spirit fails; but stretching forth Thy lofty arm, O Master, save me, O Helmsman, even as Peter.*[^3121]

*Troparion 1:* In word and deed and action there is none that has lived wickedly upon earth as I; therefore do I ask for thy love of mankind, O Lady, that I may obtain mercy.[^3122]

*Troparion 2:* The deeds of evil transgressions give birth to sorrows for all; therefore by bitter afflictions are we now beset. Help us, O thou who alone art the salvation of Christians.[^3123]

*Glory:* Thou both givest birth and hast appeared pure after childbirth, O Virgin Theotokos! Therefore ever pray to Him Who was born of thee, that we thy servants may be delivered from every harm of the enemy.[^3124]

*Both now:* By the prayers of Thy most pure Mother, and of all the Prophets and Martyrs, and of the holy Disciples, O Word of God, grant us peace and cleansing of transgressions.[^3125]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3:*
All my life have I passed in great slothfulness, O Most Pure One; and now have I drawn near unto the departure of my time, and am terrified of my foes, lest they tear my soul apart, O All-immaculate One, and cast me into the pit of perdition; but show compassion unto thy servant, O Virgin, and deliver me from their malice.[^3126]

*Tone 3, Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*As of old Thou didst bedew the three pious youths in the Chaldean flame with the bright fire of the Godhead, so also illumine us who cry aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.*[^3127]

*Troparion 1:* Defiled in body and soul, and wretched with unclean deeds, I pray thee, O pure and undefiled Mother of God, O Lady, trusting in thy mercy: show compassion on me, O Most Pure One.[^3128]

*Troparion 2:* The multitude of my transgressions and the trial of evils exceedingly confound me with perplexity, and drag me down into the abyss of despair; but do thou, O most pure Lady...

*On Wednesday at Small Compline, Tone 3.*

...save me who am perishing and grievously drowning.[^3129]

*Glory:* By the multitude of the compassions of Thy goodness, overlook the multitude of our sins, O Good One, and be easily reconciled unto those who unceasingly cry: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.[^3130]

*Both now:* From the mire of passions, and the depth of afflictions, and worldly tribulations, draw us unto dispassion, O Pure One, and unto joyful gladness, O Blessed One, who cry: Thou alone art full of divine grace.[^3131]

*Tone 3, Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The flame of material fire did the God-beholding youths subdue with the immaterial fire, and sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^3132]

*Troparion 1:* The bitterness of pleasures having passed through all my bodily senses, foully defiles my soul and drags it into death; O Lady of the world, be my salvation.[^3133]

*Troparion 2:* Upon thee have I placed soul and heart and body; for I have no other hope save thee, O Lady, through whom I may obtain mercy; therefore grant me thy rich mercy and grace.[^3134]

*Glory:* The serpent, having crept near, has grievously slain my wretched soul with the poison of carnal pleasure; but make it alive with contrary remedies, O Pure One, through thy prayers.[^3135]

*Both now:* O thou who gavest birth to God and the Savior, pray Him, the Prince of Peace, to send peace swiftly to the world, that in peace we may glorify Him.[^3136]

*Tone 3, Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Thee, the unburnt bush, and holy Virgin, Mother of the Light, and Theotokos, the hope of us all, do we magnify.*[^3137]

*Troparion 1:* Cleanse the defilement of the passionate thoughts of my mind, O Pure One, clothing me in the bright garment of dispassion.[^3138]

*Troparion 2:* Open unto me, O Virgin, the divine entrances of repentance, blocking the entrances of my passions and pleasures and forbidding them by thy power.[^3139]

*Glory:* Hear the voice of my groaning, and the voice of my weeping, and grant cleansing and salvation unto my wretched soul, O all-immaculate Virgin.[^3140]

*Both now:* Daniel the Prophet beheld thy birthgiving, O Virgin; he truly beheld...

*On Wednesday at Small Compline, Tone 3.*

...truly on the throne, Whom he proclaimed as the Ancient of Days.[^3141]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^3142]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*
*After the first Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Come, all ye people, let us praise the Apostles as helmsmen; for they abolished the delusion of idols, and led us unto the light of life, and taught us to believe in the Trinity. Therefore today, celebrating their memory with honor, we the faithful glorify Christ our God.[^3143]

*Verse: Into all the earth went out their sound, and their words unto the ends of the world.*[^3144]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The memory of Thine Apostles hast Thou made illustrious, O Lord, as Almighty, in that Thou didst strengthen them to be conformed unto Thy passions; for manfully did they conquer the enemy's power, therefore also they received the grace of healings. By their prayers, O Lover of mankind, grant peace unto Thy people.[^3145]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Taking into mind the immeasurable wealth of thy tender compassion and the invincible power of thy might, I have fled unto thy protection; held fast by sorrow, and grievously perplexed, I cry aloud, and from the depth of my heart with tears, O Virgin Theotokos, I cry unto thee: Save me, save me, unworthy one.[^3146]

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* The Apostles watered all the earth with streams of the Word of God, and the ear of faith grew up, and filled the ends of the universe; for they cut down all tares, and brought all men unto Christ God, baptizing them into the uncreated Trinity.[^3147]

*Verse: The heavens declare the glory of God, and the firmament proclaims the work of His hands.*[^3148]

*And again we say the same Sessional Hymn.*[^3149]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3150]

*Martyrikon:* O holy Passion-bearers, entreat the merciful God, that He may grant remission of transgressions unto our souls.[^3151]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* The Prophets proclaimed, the Apostles taught, the Martyrs confessed, and we have believed thee to be truly the Theotokos; therefore...

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...and we magnify thy Childbirth, O Most Pure One.[^3152]

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3:*
*Sessional Hymn 1:* Thine Apostles, O Christ, hast Thou sent forth unto the ends of the earth as the Merciful One, as sharp, mighty arrows, slaying all ungodliness and evil counsel, and planting saving teachings. By their supplications, O Compassionate One, grant peace unto our souls.[^3153]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Praising thee as a preacher of truth and an all-radiant luminary of the world, we sing to thee, O Nicholas, and cry in faith, praying: As thou didst snatch away the innocent from death, so also, O Holy One, deliver us from troubles and afflictions and every grievous oppression.[^3154]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Make alive my soul which has been slain by many transgressions, O all-immaculate Lady, as one having maternal boldness before thy Son; for thou alone didst bear beyond mind and comprehension the Word Who is co-unoriginate with the Father and the Spirit, Who ever grants life and incorruption to the world, and great mercy.[^3155]

*Canon to the Holy, Glorious, and All-Praised Apostles.*
*(A composition of Theophanes.)*
*Tone 3, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by His divine nodding gathered the waters together into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; to Him alone do we sing, for He has been glorified.*[^3156]

*Troparion 1:* Pillars of the Church, foundations of the faith, pious confirmations that strengthen all the faithful: come, let us call the holy Apostles blessed, that by their supplications we may be saved. (Twice)[^3157]

*Troparion 2:* O immovable pillars of piety! Tripped up by the deceit of the enemy, and lying upon the earth in affliction, set me upright, that I may find remission for the things in which I have sinned.[^3158]

*Troparion 3:* Prodigal that I am, before the judgment I fear and condemn myself; for countless evil deeds have I acquired. Therefore I pray Thee, O righteous Judge: save me who am in despair, by the divine supplications of Thine Apostles.[^3159]

*Theotokion:* With the holy Prophets, with the Apostles, and the Martyrs, earnestly entreat the Lord Who was incarnate from thee, O Pure One, that He may put to death all our carnal passions, and grant us eternal life.[^3160]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Another Canon to the Holy and Great Wonderworker Nicholas.*
*(A composition of Joseph.)*
*Tone 3, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Let us sing unto the Lord, Who wrought marvelous wonders in the Red Sea; for He covered the adversaries with a flood, and saved Israel. To Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.*[^3161]

*Troparion 1:* The all-radiant, inextinguishable luminary, the pillar that is upon earth, who calls unto the divine harbor those who are ever drowned by the waves of this life: let us all honor Nicholas, and in love let us call him blessed.[^3162]

*Troparion 2:* Strengthened by the power of God, O All-blessed One, thou didst acquire zeal for piety according to knowledge; therefore thou didst deliver those who were about to die unjustly. For this cause we entreat thee: deliver us from all unjust affliction, O Nicholas.[^3163]

*Troparion 3:* Offer supplications unto the Lord unceasingly, O father, that He may deliver us from sins and from the flame of eternal torment, and from tribulations and afflictions, as the One Good.[^3164]

*Theotokion:* The divine ark that contained the Lawgiver, Who takes away all our iniquities for the sake of the ineffable abyss of His divine mercy: Mary the most pure, let us sing with one accord.[^3165]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who from nothingness broughtest all things into being, which are created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit: O Almighty Master, establish me in Thy love.*[^3166]

*Troparion 1:* Ye who have filled the ends of the world with divine teaching, fill my soul with virtuous deeds and good thoughts, O eyewitnesses of Christ. (Twice)[^3167]

*Troparion 2:* From the many stumbling blocks of the crafty one, and from every sorrow, and from every straitness, from every besetting distress, O Disciples, deliver me.[^3168]

*Troparion 3:* Woe is me, O my wretched and humble soul! Often sinning and provoking God to anger, how wilt thou ask forgiveness of the evils which thou ceasest not to commit?[^3169]

*Theotokion:* O divine ladder which Jacob beheld, upon which God came down, lifting us up: earnestly pray with the Apostles that He have compassion on us.[^3170]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O barren and childless soul, acquire glorious and well-favored...*

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...fruit, and rejoicing cry aloud: I have been established by Thee, O God! There is none holy, there is none righteous beside Thee, O Lord.[^3171]

*Troparion 1:* A sharp spiritual sword didst thou appear, O Wise One, cutting down the evil tares of the heretics, and smoothing out the saving paths of virtues for the faithful, O father Nicholas.[^3172]

*Troparion 2:* Unto thee, the fountain that pours forth streams of healings, do we pray, O Holy One: wash away the defilement of the passions of us all by thy prayers, delivering us from troubles and afflictions.[^3173]

*Troparion 3:* Casting down the enemy by thy mighty prayers, now again strengthen our thoughts that are weakened by passionate assaults, through the invocation of thy holy prayers, O father Nicholas.[^3174]

*Theotokion:* The mind cannot comprehend the birthgiving beyond mind of thy marvelous Childbirth, O all-immaculate Parent of God! Therefore I pray thee: endow my mind with grace, that I may ever glorify thee.[^3175]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown steadfast love toward us, O Lord; for Thou gavest Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore with thanksgiving we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3176]

*Troparion 1:* As lightning and as rays of the unwaning Sun, spreading forth Thine Apostles unto the ends of the world, O Word, having enlightened all, Thou didst drive away the darkness of godlessness. (Twice)[^3177]

*Troparion 2:* The sea of grievous sin roars against me, O Word, and the billows of countless thoughts trouble my humble soul; O Helmsman Lord, by the supplications of Thy Disciples save me.[^3178]

*Troparion 3:* I am terrified when I consider Thy dread coming, O Master; for before the judgment I have my conscience within condemning me, and before the torment my insensibility torments me.[^3179]

*Theotokion:* O Word of God, Who wast born of the Virgin, the Handmaid of God! By her holy intercessions, and of Thy most wise Apostles, O Savior, deliver our souls from every besetting distress and every constraint.[^3180]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Habakkuk foresaw the overshadowed mountain, even thy most pure womb, O Pure One; therefore he cried aloud: God shall come from Theman, and the Holy One from the mountain overshadowed and densely wooded.*[^3181]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 1:* Every believer ever puts thee forward as an intercessor before God, O Nicholas. Therefore we entreat thee: deliver us from grievous tribulations and falls into sin, O father.[^3182]

*Troparion 2:* Having living water in thy heart, the grace of the Lord, thou dost ever bedew, O Wise One, those who are wretchedly distressed by the heat of sorrows and the drought of sins, and are about to perish, O All-blessed One.[^3183]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst of old deliver those being led unjustly unto death, O All-blessed One: save us now also from the oppression of corrupt men, and from all demonic deceit.[^3184]

*Theotokion:* The incarnate Word, through the exceeding greatness of His tender compassion, didst thou bear ineffably; pray Him, O Most Pure One, to deliver all from carnal passions and defilements, and from all constraints of this life.[^3185]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Upon earth Thou, the Invisible One, didst appear, and willingly didst dwell among men, O Incomprehensible One; and rising early unto Thee, we hymn Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^3186]

*Troparion 1:* Having become incarnate upon earth, Thou madest Thy Disciples as heavens declaring Thy glory, O Christ; for their sake, therefore, O Lord, have mercy on our souls. (Twice)[^3187]

*Troparion 2:* From passions and from every distress, from grievous adversity, O Word, deliver Thy servants by the acceptable prayers of Thine Apostles.[^3188]

*Troparion 3:* Woe is me, O all-passionate soul! How wilt thou stand before the dread judgment, being fruitless? Make haste, repent of evil deeds, bringing forth fruits of virtues.[^3189]

*Theotokion:* Pray Him Who ineffably appeared upon earth with flesh from thy pure blood, O Pure One, as thy Son, ever to grant us cleansing.[^3190]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*With Thine unwaning light, O Christ God, illumine my humble soul, and guide me in Thy fear, for Thy commandments are light.*[^3191]

*Troparion 1:* Standing before the pure Light, and being ever enlightened by the rays that proceed thence, O father, ask for all cleansing and peace.[^3192]

*Troparion 2:* Have compassion on me who more than all men have provoked Thee to anger with an unreasoning mind, O Most Good One, through the fervent prayers of Nicholas.[^3193]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Troparion 3:* Spending my life in despondency, I pray thee, O father Nicholas: raise up my defiled thought unto repentance.[^3194]

*Theotokion:* As the only joy of the angels, O thou who art full of divine joy, fill my mourning mind, which is overwhelmed by slothfulness, with joy.[^3195]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The lowest abyss of sins has surrounded me, and my spirit fails; but stretching forth Thy lofty arm, O Master, save me, O Helmsman, even as Peter.*[^3196]

*Troparion 1:* Having poured forth an abyss of spiritual wisdom, O choir of the Apostles, dry up the current of worldly philosophy, and water the congregations of the pious. (Twice)[^3197]

*Troparion 2:* Sigh and weep, O my humble soul, and cry aloud unto the Lord, saying: I have sinned against Thee, O Master; cleanse me, O Compassionate One, by the supplications of Thy most wise Apostles.[^3198]

*Troparion 3:* The torrents of passions have flowed, and have drowned the dwelling of my soul; having become spiritual rivers, O Apostles, renew me unto life who am broken.[^3199]

*Theotokion:* The assembly of Thine Apostles entreats Thee, O Christ, together with her that bore Thee: send down cleansing and peace unto Thy servants, as Lover of mankind and easily reconciled God.[^3200]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The depth of passions has risen against me, and the tempest of contrary winds; but anticipating me, save me, O Savior, and deliver me from corruption, even as Thou didst save the Prophet from the sea monster.*[^3201]

*Troparion 1:* Having mortified thy members through abstinence, thou didst acquire an unaging life; make us also partakers thereof by thy prayers, O Wise One, through fleeing from evil sin.[^3202]

*Troparion 2:* By the sail of thy sacred prayers, deliver us from the gulf of manifold temptations and the depths of sin, bringing us unto the harbor of life, O wise Hierarch.[^3203]

*Troparion 3:* Having adorned the throne of Myra in Lycia, thou didst appear as the beauty of high priests; but by thy prayers save us unharmed from worldly distresses, O Hierarch.[^3204]

*Theotokion:* The Word that dwelt in thy womb, O pure Mother, [has cast out] the corruption that of old dwelt among men from...[^3205]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...the corruption of transgression, as the only God, He has cast outside through the tenderheartedness of mercy.[^3206]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Of old the three youths bowed not down to the golden image, the Persian object of worship, singing in the midst of the furnace: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.*[^3207]

*Troparion 1:* Christ, Who is the Light of the world, showed you forth as lights, O light-bearing Apostles, driving away the darkness of delusion and enlightening the thoughts of the faithful. (Twice)[^3208]

*Troparion 2:* Break the enemy's snares that are set against us, O divinely speaking Apostles, and smooth for us the paths of repentance, who flee unto you.[^3209]

*Troparion 3:* Cleanse my soul that has lost its savor through carnal passions, O divinely eloquent Apostles of the Lord, being divine salt, reviving it by faith.[^3210]

*Theotokion:* O Beauty of Jacob, illumine me with goodly appearances, I pray thee, entreating now together with the Apostles Him Who was born of thy pure blood.[^3211]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The three youths in the furnace, prefiguring the Trinity, trampled upon the fiery threat, and singing, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.*[^3212]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst strangle the foolish Arius who of old was destroying the Lord's people with the sinews of thy words, as a true chief shepherd, O father Nicholas.[^3213]

*Troparion 2:* Having holily passed through thy most holy life, thou dwellest with the Saints, O father Nicholas, sending down sanctification and enlightenment unto those who piously call thee blessed.[^3214]

*Troparion 3:* As a ready deliverer, as a fervent protector, we ever invoke thee, O father Nicholas: deliver us from passions and from unforeseen adversities that come upon us.[^3215]

*Theotokion:* Enlighten me, who through negligence have darkened my soul, O Pure One that didst bear the Light, rekindling the lamp of my heart, that with diligence in good things I may glorify thee.[^3216]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Having joined battle with the unbearable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remaining unharmed by the flame, sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^3217]

*Troparion 1:* I have been wounded by the weapon of passions, and have scabbed my heart with foolishness through the worker of malice; O glorious Apostles, heal me who am wholly at a loss...[^3218]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...being physicians of souls and bodies. (Twice)[^3219]

*Troparion 2:* Into all the earth went forth the divine utterance of the preachers, teaching orthodoxly concerning the unique Essence: to honor the one nature of the Holy Trinity, and one true Dominion, and one Kingdom unto all ages.[^3220]

*Troparion 3:* Me who have sinned greatly, and have spent Thy divine longsuffering in remaining in transgressions, turn back, O Master, knowing my weakness, O Word, and slothfulness, and evil-mindedness.[^3221]

*Theotokion:* O Theotokos, who ineffably didst receive the Divine Ember: quench the burning coals of my passions, O Most Pure One, with the dew of thy prayers and of the all-glorious and divine Apostles.[^3222]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Him Who is unceasingly glorified in the highest by the Angels as God, the heavens of heavens, the earth, and mountains, and hills, and the abyss, and all the race of men: bless Him with hymns as Creator and Deliverer, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^3223]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast inherited the land of the meek, having become meek, O Venerable One. Therefore I pray thee faithfully: by thy prayers tame the tempest of the crafty one that ever moves against me.[^3224]

*Troparion 2:* As thou didst deliver the generals who were about to die unjustly, so deliver us from the affliction of crafty men, and from every demonic assault, O Nicholas, entreating the Savior.[^3225]

*Troparion 3:* Thou hast shown unto men the right and saving path, unto which guide us who pass through this life, O Nicholas, by thy prayers, that together we may enter into the gates of life.[^3226]

*Theotokion:* My strength and song is He Who was born of thee, O Most Pure One; diligently entreat Him, O Virgin, to strengthen me who am weakened by passions, that I may do His saving commandments.[^3227]

*Canon of the Holy Apostles.*
*Tone 3, Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and writings of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: every male that opens the womb is holy unto God. Therefore the first-born Word, the unoriginate Father's Son, being first-born of a Mother that knew not man, we magnify.*[^3228]

*Troparion 1:* Thou Who by the words of Thy disciples didst establish the ends of the earth, O Word of the unoriginate Father: by their supplications, unto me also who have slipped into irrational passions...[^3229]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...and am drowned by demonic delusion, show compassion. (Twice)[^3230]

*Troparion 2:* O my soul, enslaved to the assault of passions, offer supplications unto Him Who suffered for our sake, that He may deliver thee from grievous things, as the radiant disciples entreat Him, who were likened unto His bodily sufferings.[^3231]

*Troparion 3:* O disciples of Christ, when ye sit with Him to judge those guilty of judgment, preserve uncondemned my soul, which is defiled by improper deeds, as my good champions and protectors of the world.[^3232]

*Theotokion:* O most pure Virgin, pure palace of Christ, O most holy Virgin, who didst bear the Holy of Holies, God, beyond cause and word: together with the holy Apostles, pray for us all.[^3233]

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas.*
*Tone 3, Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*On Mount Sinai Moses saw thee in the bush, having unconsumed conceived the fire of the Godhead in thy womb; and Daniel saw thee as the uncut mountain; a rod that budded, Isaiah cried aloud, from the root of David.*[^3234]

*Troparion 1:* As a divine defense and confirmation, and a good refuge, the earthly world has gained thee; for by thy mediations we are ever delivered from every temptation and distress, O father Nicholas! Therefore in praise we faithfully call thee blessed.[^3235]

*Troparion 2:* Being constrained by many circumstances, I flee unto the breadth of thy fervent prayers, O All-blessed One: transform the sicknesses of my soul, I cry unto thee; tame the waves of despair, and quiet the confusion of my mind.[^3236]

*Troparion 3:* The coming of the Creator is at hand to judge all the earth, and being unprepared, I, wretched one, am wholly terrified, considering the multitude of mine evils. O longsuffering Lord, have compassion and save me by the divine prayers of Nicholas, Thy venerable one.[^3237]

*Theotokion:* Thou hast appeared as my saving illumination, O thou who didst bear the Light, driving away the dark and grievous clouds of my soul, that by thy prayers I may become a son of the day, doing venerable things, and in hymnody venerably call thee blessed.[^3238]

*Then: It is truly meet: Litany and Exapostilarion.*[^3239]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera.*
*Tone 3.*
*Sticheron 1:* Into all the earth went forth your utterance, O holy Apostles; ye destroyed the delusion of idols, having proclaimed divine knowledge. O good was your contest, O blessed ones! Therefore we sing and glorify your memory.[^3240]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^3241]

*Sticheron 2:* Having blamelessly kept the commandments of Christ, O holy Apostles, freely ye received, freely ye bestow, healing the passions of our souls and bodies. Therefore having boldness, entreat Him that our souls may be saved.[^3242]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us; and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^3243]

*For the Martyrs:* As luminaries in the world ye shine even after death, O holy Martyrs, having fought the good fight; having boldness, entreat Christ that our souls may be saved.[^3244]

*Glory; both now: Theotokion:*
Without seed thou didst conceive of the Holy Spirit, and glorifying, we hymn thee: Rejoice, O most holy Virgin.[^3245]

*Then: It is good to give praise: and the rest.*[^3246]

*At the Liturgy:*
*The Beatitudes. Tone 3.*
*Troparion 1:* Adam our forefather, who set aside Thy commandment, O Christ, Thou didst banish from Paradise; but the thief, O Compassionate One, who confessed Thee upon the Cross, Thou didst settle therein, as he cried: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy Kingdom.[^3247]

*Troparion 2:* Ye who with the rod of the Cross drew men out of the depth of ignorance, O Apostles, and drove away the Greek delusion from the earth, were truly guileless saviors unto the faithful; therefore we call you blessed.[^3248]

*Troparion 3:* Having become mystical rays and luminaries of the Sun of righteousness, O glorious Apostles, ye drove away the darkness of impiety, and guided all people unto the light of divine knowledge; therefore we honor you.[^3249]

*Troparion 4:* Ye endured the burnings of torments, having received from on high the dew of grace, O sufferers; and human passions, as servants of Christ, ye ever piously and wisely heal. Therefore we faithfully honor you, O Saints.[^3250]

*Glory:* Sacred heralds of the Trinity, Peter, and Paul, Mark, and Luke, Matthew, Simon, James...[^3251]

*On Thursday at the Liturgy, Tone 3.*

...Andrew, John, Thomas, Bartholomew, and wise Philip! Diligently pray ever unto This Trinity, that we may be delivered from every distress.[^3252]

*Both now:* O beauty of the Apostles and rejoicing of the holy passion-bearers, O most graced Lady, pray unto the Savior and God of all for us, that we may receive remission of transgressions and all become partakers of divine life.[^3253]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^3254]

*On Thursday Evening.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 3.*
*Tone 3, Podoben: "They weighed thirty pieces of silver" (Поста́виша трї́десѧте):*

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, being impassible in Thy divine nature, Thou didst endure suffering according to Thy human nature: being nailed upon the Cross, and pierced in Thy ribs with a spear, pouring forth unto me two streams from them, of ineffable mysteries.[^3255]

*Sticheron 2:* With a crown woven of thorns Thou wast platted in mockery, O King of all, tearing asunder the threat of thorny sin, O Savior; and receiving a reed in Thy hands, in the book on high Thou hast written all of us who have believed in Thee.[^3256]

*Sticheron 3:* In nowise did the unrighteous envy of the Hebrews cease, and having crucified Thee, O Guileless One, nor when Thou wast dead, O Christ; but even so they slandered Thee as a deceiver, and the fierce ones asked Pilate to guard Thy tomb. O incurable wrath![^3257]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos. The same Tone.*
*Sticheron 1:* When she saw the unsetting Sun Who rose from her womb setting upon the Cross, holding the ray of the sun, she whose soul was weighed down with the darkness of grief cried aloud: Having set willingly, rise again, unto my enlightenment and that of the world.[^3258]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lady, who didst bear Him Who is coming to judge the living and the dead! Quicken my soul which is mortified by sickness, through repentance and through the divine blood that flowed from the ribs of thy Son, and show me forth a doer of His living commandments.[^3259]

*Sticheron 3:* When without pain I gave birth to Thee, O Child, the Ewe-lamb and Mother said, I escaped sorrows and the mist of grief; but now seeing Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O my Womb, with bitter arrows I surround my heart, and am drowned in boundless grief...[^3260]

*On Thursday Evening, Tone 3.*

...O Master.[^3261]

*Glory; both now: Stavrotheotokion:*
The world received mercy, O Word, through Thy crucifixion; creation was enlightened; the nations found salvation, O Master, cried the Most Pure One: but I now am torn asunder, seeing Thy voluntary Passion.[^3262]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera. Tone 3.*
*Sticheron 1:* We bow down before Thy precious Cross, O Christ, the guardian of the world, the salvation of us sinners, the great cleansing, the royal victory and boast of all the universe.[^3263]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, O Thou that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion on us.*[^3264]

*Sticheron 2:* Moses in the likeness of the Cross stretched out his hands on high on the mountain, and overcame Amalek; but Thou, O Savior, having stretched out Thy hands upon the precious Cross, didst embrace and save me from the enemy's bondage, and hast given me a sign of life to flee from the bow of mine adversaries. For this sake, O Word, I bow down before Thy precious Cross.[^3265]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we have been greatly filled with contempt; our soul has been exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^3266]

*Sticheron 3:* Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord; for it was planted in a place, and acts throughout the world; and it showed forth Apostles from fishermen, and Martyrs from the nations, that they may pray for our souls.[^3267]

*Glory; both now: Stavrotheotokion:*
Seeing Thee lifted up upon the Tree, O my Most Good Christ, the All-Undefiled One, weeping, cried in motherly lament: O my Most Beloved Son, how has the lawless assembly lifted Thee up upon the Cross?[^3268]

*On Thursday at Compline.*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 3.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old gathered the waters into one gathering by His divine nod, and divided the sea for the people of Israel, this is our God, Who is exceedingly glorified: unto Him alone let us sing, for He has been glorified.*[^3269]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* As the overshadowing mountain which the Prophet Habakkuk foresaw in the Spirit, I pray thee, O Most Pure One: to over-...[^3270]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...shadow me who am scorched by passions, and from the shadow of death of cruel perils deliver me.[^3271]

*Troparion 2:* By the sprinkling of the divine blood shed from the divine ribs of thy Son, O Pure One, wash the wounds of my heart, that I may magnify thee and according to duty glorify thee, who art ever-blessed and all-undefiled.[^3272]

*Glory:* Thou didst bear the Word equal in power with the Father, Who deified the nature of men; entreat Him to grant divine consolation unto me who am bewildered and exhausted by the enemy's crafts, O Pure One.[^3273]

*Both now:* Grant me cleansing of transgressions by thy divine prayers, O Virgin, for thou hast powerful prayer, O Lady; and deliver those who hymn thee from transgressions, and passions, and griefs, and distress.[^3274]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*He Who brought all things from non-being, being fashioned by the Word, perfected by the Spirit, O Almighty Ruler, confirm me in Thy love.*[^3275]

*Troparion 1:* Being merciful, O Most Pure One, entreat Him Who is ineffable in mercy and rich in compassions, to have compassion on us who are afflicted.[^3276]

*Troparion 2:* Being a temple of the Creator of all, pray that the Comforter may dwell in me, O pure Virgin, who have become a den of soul-destroying thieves.[^3277]

*Glory:* As thou didst carry in thine arms Him Who holds all things by His divine nod, O Theotokos: look upon me, and deliver me from looking upon unseemly passions.[^3278]

*Both now:* The tenderheartedness of thy mercy, I pray, open unto me, O most pure Theotokos, and appear unto me as a fervent helper and salvation in adversities.[^3279]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown steadfast love towards us, O Lord, for Thou gavest Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for our sake. Therefore we cry unto Thee, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3280]

*Troparion 1:* Having willingly endured wounds and slaughter as God, O Christ: heal my soul wounded by the assaults of demonic afflictions, by the prayers of her that bore Thee, O only Plenteous in mercy.[^3281]

*Troparion 2:* I am the work of Thy hands and Thy creation, O Fashioner; but the malice of the serpent...[^3282]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...has broken me through the pleasures of life. Therefore, O Christ, renew me by the supplications of her that bore Thee, O Word.[^3283]

*Glory:* Thou didst bear the Father's Word, beyond word, Who delivers men from all irrationality; diligently entreat Him to free me who am enslaved to irrational pleasures, O only Ever-Virgin.[^3284]

*Both now:* Thou ever pourest forth healing unto us from thy palm, O all-sanctified tabernacle, being all full of light, pouring forth fragrant myrrh unto all, O most pure Bride of God.[^3285]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*When Isaiah figuratively saw God exalted upon a throne, upborne by the Angels of glory, he cried: Woe is me, wretched one! For I have foreseen God incarnate, the Unwaning Light, and Ruler of peace.*[^3286]

*Troparion 1:* Transform the sicknesses of my flesh and the sorrow of my soul, and drive away the clouds of despondency, O Virgin, cloud of light; and grant health and relief of sicknesses unto me who sing to thee and glorify thee with love.[^3287]

*Troparion 2:* I now offer thee as mediatress and intercessor unto Him Who was born of thee, being full of every sin, O Virgin: be thou the correction of my life, and guide unto the path of divine understanding.[^3288]

*Glory:* Sanctify my mind, O Virgin, and enlighten my soul, and make me a partaker of divine glory; for behold, I am filled with evils, and have been enslaved to all manner of pleasures, and bring a defiled conscience.[^3289]

*Both now:* O divine vine, that didst bud forth the fair cluster that gives drink without begrudging unto souls, O holy Virgin Maiden: do thou give me to drink of its sweetness, and take away the drunkenness of pleasures, and save me.[^3290]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Unto those who have come unto the end of the ages, O Lover of mankind, and are in peril of perishing through the tempest of temptations, despise not those who cry aloud: Save, O Savior, even as Thou didst save the Prophet from the sea monster.*[^3291]

*Troparion 1:* I have surpassed the Pharisee who was exalted in mind, being ever puffed up, and have joined the gulfs of boundless transgressions; deliver and have compassion on me who am grievously humbled, O only Pure One.[^3292]

*Troparion 2:* Having had a most marvelous conception and birth, thy mercies...[^3293]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...show marvelously now upon me, the wretched one; for in iniquities was I conceived, and was born, and have been enslaved to pleasures.[^3294]

*Glory:* I lament, weep, and groan when I call to mind the dread judgment seat, for I have acquired crafty deeds; but stand thou by me, O Virgin Mother of God who knewest not man, in that dread hour.[^3295]

*Both now:* No mind or tongue is able to understand and utter the strange and all-glorious wonder wrought in thee, O pure Virgin: how didst thou give birth and yet remainest pure again? God is He Who was born according to essence.[^3296]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (Three times). Glory; both now:*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 3:*
Thy pure Mother who knew not wedlock, O Christ, seeing Thee hanging dead upon the Tree, weeping in motherly fashion, said: What has the lawless and ungrateful Jewish assembly repaid Thee, which enjoyed Thy many and great gifts, O my Son? I hymn Thy divine condescension.[^3297]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The three youths in the furnace, prefiguring the Trinity, trampled upon the fiery threat, and singing, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers.*[^3298]

*Troparion 1:* From works there is no salvation for me, O Lady! For I add sins to sins, and malice to malice; therefore by thy prayer, O Pure One, anticipate and save me.[^3299]

*Troparion 2:* Judgment is at the doors, and the tribunal is ready; make ready, O humble soul, and cry aloud: When Thou judgest me, O Word, condemn me not, through the prayers of her that bore Thee.[^3300]

*Glory:* Embracing the fruits of sin, I have died, and bring a barren soul, and cry unto thee, O Most Pure One: show me forth fruitful, who by thy fruit didst destroy corruption.[^3301]

*Both now:* O strange mystery! O awesome understanding! How did God appear upon earth as man? As He knows, as He wills, as He is well pleased; and He works as He wills.[^3302]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Having joined battle with the unbearable fire, the youths, champions of piety, remaining unharmed by the flame, sang the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^3303]

*Troparion 1:* Being all good, as the kinswoman unto the King of all, O Theotokos: fill...[^3304]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 3.*

...me with good works, who have lived in malice and ended all my life in slothfulness, that I may glorify thee unto all ages.[^3305]

*Troparion 2:* As Thou didst of old deliver the Prophet from the sea monster's belly all-gloriously, O Word of God, so deliver now my soul, O Savior, which has slipped into the depth of perdition, having the Virgin who without wedlock bore Thee praying unto Thee.[^3306]

*Glory:* Finding me clothed with a fair garment, the workers of malice stripped me thereof; but do thou thyself, O Virgin Mother of God, adorn me with the divine garments of repentance by thy prayers.[^3307]

*Both now:* Him before Whom all creation trembles, O Pure One, thou didst hold in thine arms, Who for our sake became an infant through tenderheartedness; entreat Him to save all who cry aloud in faith: We exalt thee, O Most Pure One, unto all ages.[^3308]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*In the shadow and writings of the Law, we the faithful see a figure: every male that opens the womb is holy unto God. Therefore the first-born Word, the unoriginate Father's Son, being first-born of a Mother that knew not man, we magnify.*[^3309]

*Troparion 1:* Rotting in mind and soul, having defiled my conscience with malice, and appearing stripped of all good things, O incorrupt undefiled Virgin, despise me not, but adorn me with the deeds of piety.[^3310]

*Troparion 2:* I am filled with evils, I am filled with thoughts that estrange me from Thee, O Lover of mankind; therefore I groan and cry aloud: Receive me who repent, and cast me not away, through the supplications of her that bore Thee, O plenteous in mercy Benefactor.[^3311]

*Glory:* May I be delivered by thy prayers, O all-undefiled Maiden, from all wrath, and deadly passions, and the fierce fire of Gehenna, from unrighteous men and from evil foes, who have fled unto thy shelter and call upon thee for help.[^3312]

*Both now:* Finding thee fair in soul, fair in thoughts, fair in body, the Most Fair One was incarnate from thy virginal loins, as He knows, adorning our ugliness, O Virgin; entreat Him that we may be saved.[^3313]

*Then: It is truly meet: Trisagion, and after Our Father: the rest as usual, and dismissal.*[^3314]

*On Friday at Matins.*[^3315]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 3.*
The Cross was planted upon the earth, and touched heaven; not that the wood reached height, but Thou upon it, Who fillest all things. O Lord, glory to Thee.[^3316]

*Verse:* Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.[^3317]

Having willed to suffer the Cross and death, Thou didst endure these in the midst of creation; for when Thou wast well-pleased, O Savior, to nail Thy Body, then also the sun hid its rays; then also the robber, beholding these things, praised Thee upon the cross, crying aloud devoutly: Remember me, O Lord! and having believed, received paradise.[^3318]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion.*
The undefiled Ewe-lamb of the Word, the incorrupt Virgin-Mother, seeing suspended upon the Cross Him Who from her without pain had sprouted forth, wailing in motherly fashion, cried aloud: Woe is me, my Child! How sufferest Thou willingly, desiring to deliver man from the passions of dishonor?[^3319]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3.*
Upon cypress, and pine, and cedar, Thou wast lifted up, O Lamb of God, that Thou mightest save those who with faith worship Thy voluntary crucifixion. O Christ God, glory to Thee.[^3320]

*Verse:* But God is our King before the ages, He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.[^3321]

At Thine unsearchable power and voluntary crucifixion the angelic hosts marvelled, seeing how the Invisible One received wounds in the flesh, desiring to deliver mankind from corruption. Therefore, as unto our Benefactor, we cry aloud unto Thee: Glory, O Christ, to Thy tenderheartedness.[^3322]

*Verse:* God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.[^3323]

*Martyricon:*
The noble endurance of your patience vanquished the wiles of the evil-originating foe, O all-praised martyrs! Therefore ye were accounted worthy of eternal beatitude. But pray ye unto the Lord, that He save the flock of Christ-loving people, being witnesses of the truth.[^3324]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion.*
A shameful death in crucifixion Thou didst endure willingly, O Compassionate One; and she that bore Thee, O Christ, seeing Thee, was wounded. By her supplications, through the tenderheartedness of Thy mercy, O only All-good, Lover of mankind, O Lord, have compassion, and save the world, Thou Who takest away the sins of the world.[^3325]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 3.*
Having endured the Cross, O Sinless One, the vessel of the curse, through Thine ineffable compassion, Thou didst deliver the first-created from the first curse; therefore we worship Thy precious Passion, glorifying Thy holy dispensation, which Thou alone, having fulfilled through the tenderheartedness of mercy, hast saved Thy creation.[^3326]

Having been struck upon the cheek for the human race, and having not been angered: deliver our life from corruption, O Lord, and have mercy upon us, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^3327]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion.*
The heavenly hosts, seeing it, were struck with awe, and earthly beings trembled with fear, when Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross willingly, O Sinless One! The sun was clothed in darkness, and the moon was obscured. Then also Thy Mother, grieving, said: O my Son, what is this sight? How hast Thou, the One, endured for all?[^3328]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*
*(Whose acrostic is: The Cross is the boast and glory of the faithful.)*
*(A Creation of Joseph.)*[^3329]

*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*The sun once traversed the dry land that was deep-generating; for like a wall the water congealed on both sides for the people passing over the sea on foot, and singing in a manner pleasing unto God: Let us sing unto the Lord, for He is gloriously glorified.*[^3330]

*Refrain: Glory to Thy Cross and Crucifixion, O Lord.*[^3331]

*Troparion 1:* Having congealed the sea waves with a rod, Thou didst lead through the people, prefiguring Thy Cross, whereby Thou didst cleave the deceitful water, and unto the land of good understanding, O Compassionate One, Thou didst bring all who sing of Thy power with faith.[^3332]

*Troparion 2:* He Who made the abyss by His command, and Who mightily covers the high places with waters, and Who suspended the earth upon the waters: Thou hangest upon the Wood, and shakest all creation by Thy nod, yet dost establish the hearts of all in Thy fear.[^3333]

*Martyricon 1:* Ye became divine sons by communion, O martyrs of Christ, and citizens and heirs of the Sion on high, in which also, wearing crowns, ye cry aloud radiantly: Let us sing unto the Lord, for He is gloriously glorified.[^3334]

*Martyricon 2:* Having undergone the severing of hands and feet, O martyrs, and being scraped fiercely, and mingled with fire, ye by no means denied Christ, Who is the God of all, but fervently cried aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord, for He is gloriously glorified.[^3335]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 3.*

*Theotokion:*
O all-good Virgin Lady! Seeing lifted up willingly upon the Wood the Son Whom thou didst bear, weeping thou didst cry aloud, calling out with pain: O God of all, tenderhearted, being the Lord of glory, how sufferest Thou these things, O Master?[^3336]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^3337]

*Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*He Who of old by divine beck gathered the waters into one gathering, and divided the sea for the people of Israel: this is our God, and He is exceedingly glorified; unto Him alone let us sing, for He is glorified.*[^3338]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^3339]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me cleansing of transgressions by thy divine supplications, O Virgin; for thou hast powerful prayer, O Most Pure One, and dost deliver those who honor thee from sins, and passions, and afflictions, and adversities.[^3340]

*Troparion 2:* With the waters of thy prayers, O Virgin, bedew my humble soul which is melted with the scorching heat of my boundless sins and passions, that having obtained divine refreshment, I may magnify thee with hymns, as a fervent protectress.[^3341]

*Troparion 3:* Me who am wholly submerged in sins, and am wholly in despair, do thou, O pure Lady, stretching forth the hand of thy compassion, draw up unto the height of repentance, granting me a fountain of tears.[^3342]

*Troparion 4:* Tear asunder, O Most Pure One, the sinful handwritten bond of my transgressions by thy prayers, as thou hast boldness toward thy Son to pray diligently for us; for thee alone we Christians have as champion.[^3343]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*The confirmation of them that hope in Thee: confirm, O Lord, the Church, which Thou hast purchased with Thy precious Blood.*[^3344]

*Troparion 1:* Bearing one composite hypostasis, O Word, Thou didst endure a most dishonorable crucifixion, which became the cause of honor unto them that honor Thee.[^3345]

*Troparion 2:* Earthborn mortals were released from the curse, when Thou, O Master, becamest a curse, and didst pour forth blessing by the Cross.[^3346]

*Martyricon 1:* Having been sojourners upon all the earth, ye were shown forth truly heavenly citizens, and joint-heirs with Christ, O all-praised ones.[^3347]

*Martyricon 2:* Having acquired the Cross as a most mighty weapon, O martyrs, the whole power of the destructive...[^3348]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...foe they vanquished unto the end.[^3349]

*Theotokion:*
Thou wast preserved a virgin even after childbirth, O pure one, having borne God incarnate, Who was nailed upon the Cross of His own will.[^3350]

*Another Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Master, Who didst bring all things out of non-existence, which are created by the Word and perfected by the Spirit: establish me in Thy love.*[^3351]

*Troparion 1:* Open unto me quickly, I pray, the tender compassion of thy mercy, O most pure Theotokos, and appear unto me as a fervent helper in temptations, and salvation.[^3352]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me, thy servant, O Most Pure One, from every raging, grievous storm of sin that holds me fast, and direct me unto the saving harbor by thy prayers.[^3353]

*Troparion 3:* Save me, O pure Virgin Mother, from the turbid minglings of my passions which now surround and afflict my humble soul.[^3354]

*Troparion 4:* Grant me a cloud of tears, O Good One, and with these extinguish the furnace of my passions, and wash away every foul defilement of my soul, O Theotokos.[^3355]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Thy virtue covered the heavens, O Christ; for having come forth from the ark of Thy sanctification, Thine incorrupt Mother, Thou didst appear in the temple of Thy glory as an infant borne in arms, and all things were filled with Thy praise.*[^3356]

*Troparion 1:* Thou alone didst raise up the fall of Adam, O Christ, having become the new Adam, Who didst nail Thy hands upon the Cross; and beaten with a reed of Thine own will, Thou didst taste vinegar and gall, Who art exalted above all by the height of Thy kingdom.[^3357]

*Troparion 2:* As a sheep to the slaughter the Prophet foresaw Thee, and as a lamb, O Word of God, not opposing at all, nor crying out; for of Thine own will Thou didst endure to be crucified, that Thou mightest deliver and save those who sinned willingly, O merciful Lord.[^3358]

*Martyricon 1:* Having tilled their souls with the plow of faith, the martyrs of Christ sowed the seed through endurance of torments, and reaped the abundant ear of martyrdom, which nourishes the sustenance of the faithful; therefore they are ever praised.[^3359]

*Martyricon 2:* Pressed hard by the affliction of intolerable cruelties, by the expectation of good things, unto the breadth of the kingdom on high the martyrs manifestly...[^3360]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...attained, that they might widen our mouth unceasingly to hymn their struggles.[^3361]

*Theotokion:*
Thou didst receive the divine Coal, as tongs, which by no means burned, but rather bedewed, O Virgin Mother, thy seedless and divine womb; Whom seeing suspended in the flesh willingly upon the Wood, thou didst glorify with hymns.[^3362]

*Another Heirmos:*
*Thou hast shown toward us steadfast love, O Lord; for Thou didst give Thine Only-begotten Son unto death for us. Therefore we cry unto Thee, giving thanks: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3363]

*Troparion 1:* I have placed all my hope upon thee, O Lady; falling down from my soul, I pray: deliver me from deadly disease, raising me up unto the life of salvation, O thou that didst bear Life.[^3364]

*Troparion 2:* O pure Lady, strong helper of the world! Cast me not away from thee, nor send me away put to shame from thy face, nor show me forth as a rejoicing unto the demons.[^3365]

*Troparion 3:* I, wretched one, have been stripped entirely of divine deeds, and have been wounded with the sharp arrows of lusts, and covered with scabs. Therefore I cry aloud unto thee, O Lady: Save me, O Most Pure One.[^3366]

*Troparion 4:* The waters of unfitting deeds have reached even unto my wretched soul, O Pure One, and held fast by earthly thoughts, I cry unto thee with pain: Despise not, O Lady, thy servant.[^3367]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*As Isaiah symbolically saw God upon an exalted throne, borne aloft by angels of glory, O wretched me! he cried aloud: for I have foreseen God incarnate, ruling with unwaning light and peace.*[^3368]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast given rest unto me, O Word, who was overburdened with the labor of transgressions, when Thou didst rest upon the Wood, O Master; and Thou didst take away my reproach by the reproaches which Thou, O Jesus, didst suffer. I hymn Thy dominion and Thy divine Passion.[^3369]

*Troparion 2:* Having kindled Thy flesh upon the Cross as a lamp, Thou didst search for the lost drachma, O Lover of mankind, and Thou summonest all Thy friends, the heavenly powers, to its finding. We hymn the dominion of Thy kingdom, O Christ.[^3370]

*Martyricon:* Cast down at the feet of the guileless passion-bearers of Christ, the deceiver is seen dead and inactive; while they themselves in guilelessness are numbered with...[^3371]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 3.*

...the angels, being filled with ineffable joy.[^3372]

*Martyricon:* The holy ones, greatly frozen by cold and cruel torments and tribulations and wounds, truly passed over unto the divine warmth of the heavenly kingdom, and are ever shown forth as fervent advocates for the faithful.[^3373]

*Theotokion:* Seeing Him Who of old fashioned Eve from the ribs crucified upon the Wood and His ribs dug into with a spear, the most pure Virgin cried aloud in motherly fashion: How dost Thou die, O my Son, O immortal Life?[^3374]

*Another Heirmos, the same:*
